Exterior walls required to be fire-resistance rated in accordance with Section 1021 for exterior egress balconies, Section 1023.7 for interior exit stairways and ramps, Section 1024.8 for exit passageways and Section 1027.6 for exterior exit stair- ways and ramps. 2. Exterior walls required to be fire-resistance rated in accordance with Section 1207 of the Fire Code of New York State for enclosure of energy storage systems. | drafted:general.bc_707_4_exterior_walls |
| 707.5 | Continuity | flag | draft | Fire barriers run from the floor below to the deck above, through concealed spaces, with exceptions for enclosures ending at a top enclosure: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_5_continuity). | 707.5 Continuity. Fire barriers shall extend from the top of the foundation or floor/ceiling assembly below to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, slab or deck above and shall be securely attached thereto. Such fire barriers shall be continuous through concealed space, such as the space above a suspended ceiling. Joints and voids at intersections shall comply with Sections 707.8 and 707.9 Exceptions: 1. Shaft enclosures shall be permitted to terminate at a top enclosure complying with Section 713.12. 2. Interior exit stairway and ramp enclosures required by Section 1023 and exit access stairway and ramp enclosures required by Section 1019 shall be permitted to terminate at a top enclosure complying with Section 713.12. 3. An exit passageway enclosure required by Section 1024.3 that does not extend to the underside of the floor sheathing, roof sheathing, slab or deck above shall be enclosed at the top with construction of the same fire-resistance rating as required for the exit passageway. | drafted:general.bc_707_5_continuity |
| 707.5.1 | Supporting construction | flag | draft | The construction supporting a fire barrier has its rating, and hollow spaces in it are fireblocked: the structure isn't in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_5_1_supporting_construction). | 707.5.1 Supporting construction. The supporting construction for a fire barrier shall be protected to afford the required fire- resistance rating of the fire barrier supported. Hollow vertical spaces within a fire barrier shall be fireblocked in accordance with Section 718.2 at every floor level. Exceptions: 1. The maximum required fire-resistance rating for assemblies supporting fire barriers separating tank storage as provided for in Section 415.9.1.2 shall be 2 hours, but not less than required by Table 601 for the building construction type. 2. Supporting construction for 1-hour fire barriers required by Table 509.1 in buildings of Types IIB, IIIB and VB construc- tion is not required to be fire-resistance rated unless required by other sections of this code. | drafted:general.bc_707_5_1_supporting_construction |
| 707.6 | Openings | flag | draft | Openings in fire barriers are protected per Section 716 and limited to 25 percent of the wall's length and 156 square feet each, with exceptions: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_6_openings). | 707.6 Openings. Openings in a fire barrier shall be protected in accordance with Section 716. Openings shall be limited to a maxi- mum aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall, and the maximum area of any single opening shall not exceed 156 square feet (15 m[fn2]). Openings in enclosures for shafts, interior exit stairways and ramps and exit passageways shall also comply with Sections 713.7, 1023.4 and 1024.5, respectively. Exceptions: 1. Openings shall not be limited to 156 square feet (15 m[fn2]) where adjoining floor areas are equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1. 2. Openings shall not be limited to 156 square feet (15 m[fn2]) or an aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall where the opening protective is a fire door serving enclosures for exit access stairways and ramps, and interior exit stair- ways and ramps. 3. Openings shall not be limited to 156 square feet (15 m[fn2]) or an aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall where the opening protective has been tested in accordance with ASTM E119 or UL 263 and has a minimum fire-resistance rating not less than the fire-resistance rating of the wall. 4. Fire window assemblies permitted in atrium separation walls shall not be limited to a maximum aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall. 5. Openings shall not be limited to 156 square feet (15 m[fn2]) or an aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall where the opening protective is a fire door assembly in a fire barrier separating an enclosure for exit access stairways and ramps, and interior exit stairways and ramps from an exit passageway in accordance with Section 1023.3.1. 6. Openings providing entrance to an elevator car shall not be limited to 156 square feet (15 m[fn2]) or an aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall where the opening protective is a fire door assembly in a fire barrier that is an elevator hoistway enclosure. 7. Openings shall not be limited to an aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall where opening serves a shaft enclosure in accordance with Section 713. 8. Openings shall not be limited to an aggregate width of 25 percent of the length of the wall where opening serves a chute access room in accordance with Section 713.13.3 or a chute discharge room in accordance with Section 713.13.4. | drafted:general.bc_707_6_openings |
| 707.7 | Penetrations | flag | draft | Penetrations of fire barriers meet Section 714 (brought in only in part: 714.1 to 714.5): not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_7_penetrations). | 707.7 Penetrations. Penetrations of fire barriers shall comply with Section 714. | drafted:general.bc_707_7_penetrations |
| 707.7.1 | Prohibited penetrations | flag | draft | Penetrations into shaft, exit stairway and exit passageway enclosures only where 713.8.1, 1023.5 and 1024.6 allow: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_7_1_prohibited_penetrations). | 707.7.1 Prohibited penetrations. Penetrations into enclosures for shafts, interior exit stairways and ramps, and exit passage- ways shall be allowed only where permitted by Sections 713.8.1, 1023.5 and 1024.6, respectively. | drafted:general.bc_707_7_1_prohibited_penetrations |
| 707.8 | Joints | flag | draft | Joints in and at fire barriers meet Section 715 (not brought in): not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_8_joints). | 707.8 Joints. Joints made in or between fire barriers, and joints made at the intersection of fire barriers with the underside of a fire- resistance-rated floor or roof sheathing, slab or deck above, and with other fire-resistance-rated wall assemblies shall comply with Section 715. | drafted:general.bc_707_8_joints |
| 707.9 | Voids at intersections | flag | draft | Voids where a fire barrier meets an unrated roof or exterior wall meet Section 715: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_9_voids). | 707.9 Voids at intersections. The voids created at the intersection of a fire barrier and a nonfire-resistance-rated roof assembly or a nonfire-resistance-rated exterior wall assembly shall comply with Section 715. | drafted:general.bc_707_9_voids |
| 707.10 | Ducts and air transfer openings | flag | draft | Ducts and air transfer openings through fire barriers meet Section 717 (not brought in): not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_707_10_ducts). | 707.10 Ducts and air transfer openings. Penetrations in a fire barrier by ducts and air transfer openings shall comply with Section 717. | drafted:general.bc_707_10_ducts |
| 708.1 | General | flag | draft | These walls are fire partitions and meet Section 708: dwelling and sleeping unit separations in Groups I-1 and R (420.2), tenant separations in covered and open mall buildings (402.4.2.1), corridor walls (the book's words name 1020.3; 1020.2 sends rated corridor walls here), enclosed elevator lobbies (3006.3), egress balconies (1021.2), walls around ambulatory care facilities (422.2), walls between dwelling and sleeping units in Groups R-1 and R-2 (907.2.8.1, 907.2.9.1) and vestibules (1028.2): a person's check (general.bc_708_1_which_walls_are_fire_partitions). | 708.1 General. The following wall assemblies shall comply with this section: 1. Separation walls as required by Section 420.2 for Group I-1 and Group R occupancies. 2. Walls separating tenant spaces in covered and open mall buildings as required by Section 402.4.2.1. 3. Corridor walls as required by Section 1020.3. 4. Enclosed elevator lobby separation as required by Section 3006.3. 5. Egress balconies as required by Section 1021.2 6. Walls separating ambulatory care facilities from adjacent spaces, corridors or tenants as required by Section 422.2. 7. Walls separating dwelling and sleeping units in Groups R-1 and R-2 in accordance with Sections 907.2.8.1 and 907.2.9.1. 8. Vestibules in accordance with Section 1028.2. | drafted:general.bc_708_1_which_walls_are_fire_partitions |
| 708.3 | Fire-resistance rating | flag | draft | A fire partition is rated at least 1 hour, except corridor walls Table 1020.2 lets be rated 1/2 hour, and dwelling and sleeping unit separations in Types IIB, IIIB and VB, which may be 1/2 hour in a building sprinklered throughout per 903.3.1.1: a person's check (general.bc_708_3_fire_partition_rating). | 708.3 Fire-resistance rating. Fire partitions shall have a fire-resistance rating of not less than 1 hour. Exceptions: 1. Corridor walls permitted to have a 1/2-hour fire-resistance rating by Table 1020.2. 2. Dwelling unit and sleeping unit separations in buildings of Types IIB, IIIB and VB construction shall have fire-resistance ratings of not less than 1/2 hour in buildings equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1. | drafted:general.bc_708_3_fire_partition_rating |
| 708.4 | Continuity | flag | draft | A fire partition runs from the foundation or floor below to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above, or to a floor/ceiling or roof/ceiling assembly rated at least as the partition, fastened to it; except above a crawl space whose floor is rated at least 1 hour; a corridor wall whose ceiling membrane equals the wall's, where the room side of the wall reaches a rated floor or roof above, or the building is sprinklered throughout per 903.3.1.1 or 903.3.1.2 with sprinklers above the partition; a corridor wall ending at the upper membrane of a ceiling built as the wall; and mall tenant separations per 402.4.2.1, which may stop at the ceiling: a person's check (general.bc_708_4_fire_partition_continuity). | 708.4 Continuity. Fire partitions shall extend from the top of the foundation or floor/ceiling assembly below and be securely attached to one of the following: 1. The underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above. 2. The underside of a floor/ceiling or roof/ceiling assembly having a fire-resistance rating that is not less than the fire-resis- tance rating of the fire partition. Exceptions: 1. Fire partitions shall not be required to extend into a crawl space below where the floor above the crawl space has a mini- mum 1-hour fire-resistance rating. 2. Fire partitions serving as a corridor wall shall not be required to extend above the lower membrane of a corridor ceiling provided that the corridor ceiling membrane is equivalent to corridor wall membrane, and either of the following condi- tions is met: 2.1. The room-side membrane of the corridor wall extends to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab of a fire-resistance-rated floor or roof above. 2.2. The building is equipped with an automatic sprinkler system installed throughout in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 or 903.3.1.2, including automatic sprinklers installed in the space between the top of the fire partition and underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above. 3. Fire partitions serving as a corridor wall shall be permitted to terminate at the upper membrane of the corridor ceiling assembly where the corridor ceiling is constructed as required for the corridor wall. 4. Fire partitions separating tenant spaces in a covered or open mall building complying with Section 402.4.2.1 shall not be required to extend above the underside of a ceiling. Such ceiling shall not be required to be part of a fire-resistance-rated assembly, and the attic or space above the ceiling at tenant separation walls shall not be required to be subdivided by fire partitions. | drafted:general.bc_708_4_fire_partition_continuity |
| 708.4.1 | Fire partition walls enclosing elevator lobbies | flag | draft | Fire partitions enclosing an elevator lobby per 3006.3 form an enclosure ending at a fire barrier or fire partition rated at least 1 hour, or an outside wall: a person's check (general.bc_708_4_1_elevator_lobby_fire_partitions). | 708.4.1 Fire partition walls enclosing elevator lobbies. Fire partition walls used to enclose elevator lobbies in accordance with Section 3006.3 shall form an enclosure that terminates at a fire barrier or fire partition having a fire-resistance rating of not less than 1 hour, or an outside wall. | drafted:general.bc_708_4_1_elevator_lobby_fire_partitions |
| 708.4.2 | Supporting construction | flag | draft | The construction supporting a fire partition is rated at least as the partition; except, in Types IIB, IIIB and VB, for partitions separating mall tenants, dwelling units, sleeping units, ambulatory care facilities from adjoining spaces or corridors, units of Groups R-1 and R-2, and vestibules from the level of exit discharge, and for corridor walls: a person's check (general.bc_708_4_2_fire_partition_support). | 708.4.2 Supporting construction. The supporting construction for a fire partition shall have a fire-resistance rating that is equal to or greater than the required fire-resistance rating of the supported fire partition. Exception: In buildings of Types IIB, IIIB and VB construction, the supporting construction requirement shall not apply to fire partitions separating tenant spaces in covered and open mall buildings, fire partitions separating dwelling units, fire partitions | drafted:general.bc_708_4_2_fire_partition_support |
| 708.4.3 | Fireblocks and draftstops in combustible construction | flag | draft | In combustible construction, where a fire partition stops short of the deck above, the space above it and along its line is fireblocked to the deck (718.2.1 materials) or draftstopped (718.3.1 for floors, 718.4.1 for attics); except a building sprinklered throughout per 903.3.1.1, or per 903.3.1.2 with sprinklers above the partition as 903.3.1.1 asks; where corridor walls separate units, a draftstop above one of them only; Group R-2 with fewer than four dwelling units, none; Group R-2 up to four stories and 60 feet above grade plane, the attic draftstopped into areas of at most 3,000 square feet or above every two units, whichever is smaller; Group R-3 with fewer than three units, none in floor assemblies: a person's check (general.bc_708_4_3_fireblocks_draftstops). | 708.4.3 Fireblocks and draftstops in combustible construction. In combustible construction where fire partitions do not extend to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above, the space above and along the line of the fire partition shall be provided with one of the following: 1. Fireblocking up to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above using materials complying with Section 718.2.1. 2. Draftstops up to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above using materials complying with Section 718.3.1 for floors or Section 718.4.1 for attics. Exceptions: 1. Buildings equipped with an automatic sprinkler system installed throughout in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1, or in accordance with Section 903.3.1.2 provided that protection is provided in the space between the top of the fire partition and underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above as required for systems complying with Section 903.3.1.1. 2. Where corridor walls provide a sleeping unit or dwelling unit separation, draftstops shall only be required above one of the corridor walls. 3. In Group R-2 occupancies with fewer than four dwelling units, fireblocking and draftstops shall not be required. 4. In Group R-2 occupancies up to and including four stories in height in buildings not exceeding 60 feet (18 288 mm) in height above grade plane, the attic space shall be subdivided by draftstops into areas not exceeding 3,000 square feet (279 m[fn2]) or above every two dwelling units, whichever is smaller. 5. In Group R-3 occupancies with fewer than three dwelling units, fireblocking and draftstops shall not be required in floor assemblies. | drafted:general.bc_708_4_3_fireblocks_draftstops |
| 708.5 | Exterior walls | flag | draft | An exterior wall that is part of a required rated separation follows Section 705 for exterior walls instead, except where 1021.2 (exterior egress balconies), 1023.7 (interior exit stairways and ramps) or 1027.6 (exterior exit stairways and ramps) asks the exterior wall itself to be rated: a person's check (general.bc_708_5_fire_partition_exterior_walls). | 708.5 Exterior walls. Where exterior walls serve as a part of a required fire-resistance-rated separation, such walls shall comply with the requirements of Section 705 for exterior walls, and the fire-resistance-rated separation requirements shall not apply. Exception: Exterior walls required to be fire-resistance rated in accordance with Section 1021.2 for exterior egress balconies, Section 1023.7 for interior exit stairways and ramps and Section 1027.6 for exterior exit stairways and ramps. | drafted:general.bc_708_5_fire_partition_exterior_walls |
| 708.6 | Openings | flag | draft | Openings in a fire partition are protected per Section 716 (Table 716.1(2): a corridor wall's doors at least 20 minutes, another fire partition's 3/4 hour in a 1-hour wall, 1/3 hour in a 1/2-hour one; Table 716.1(3): a fire window 3/4 hour (OH-45 or W-60) in a 1-hour fire partition, 1/3 hour (OH-20 or W-30) in a 1/2-hour one): a person's check (general.bc_708_6_fire_partition_openings). | 708.6 Openings. Openings in a fire partition shall be protected in accordance with Section 716. | drafted:general.bc_708_6_fire_partition_openings |
| 708.7 | Penetrations | flag | draft | Penetrations of a fire partition meet Section 714: a person's check (general.bc_708_7_fire_partition_penetrations). | 708.7 Penetrations. Penetrations of fire partitions shall comply with Section 714. | drafted:general.bc_708_7_fire_partition_penetrations |
| 708.8 | Joints | flag | draft | Joints in or between fire partitions meet Section 715: a person's check (general.bc_708_8_fire_partition_joints). | 708.8 Joints. Joints made in or between fire partitions shall comply with Section 715. | drafted:general.bc_708_8_fire_partition_joints |
| 708.9 | Ducts and air transfer openings | flag | draft | Ducts and air transfer openings through a fire partition meet Section 717: a person's check (general.bc_708_9_fire_partition_ducts). | 708.9 Ducts and air transfer openings. Penetrations in a fire partition by ducts and air transfer openings shall comply with Section 717. | drafted:general.bc_708_9_fire_partition_ducts |
| 709.3 | Fire-resistance rating | flag | draft | A smoke barrier is rated 1 hour, except one of steel at least 0.10 inch thick in a Group I-3 building. Smoke barriers enclose areas of refuge (1009.6.4) and some elevator lobbies, and divide Group I-1, I-2 and I-3 buildings and ambulatory care facilities into smoke compartments: a person's check (general.bc_709_3_smoke_barrier_rating). | 709.3 Fire-resistance rating. A 1-hour fire-resistance rating is required for smoke barriers. Exception: Smoke barriers constructed of minimum 0.10-inch-thick (2.5 mm) steel in Group I-3 buildings. | drafted:general.bc_709_3_smoke_barrier_rating |
| 709.4 | Continuity | flag | draft | A smoke barrier is an effective membrane from the foundation or floor below to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above, through concealed and interstitial spaces, its supporting construction rated as the wall or floor it supports (except in buildings of Types IIB, IIIB and VB); walls dividing smoke compartments meet 709.4.1, walls enclosing areas of refuge (1009.6.4) or elevator lobbies (405.4.3, 3007.6.2, 3008.6.2) meet 709.4.2; except none is needed in an interstitial space whose ceilings or exterior walls resist fire and smoke as well: a person's check (general.bc_709_4_smoke_barrier_continuity). | 709.4 Continuity. Smoke barriers shall form an effective membrane continuous from the top of the foundation or floor/ceiling assembly below to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above, including continuity through concealed spaces, such as those found above suspended ceilings, and interstitial structural and mechanical spaces. The supporting construction shall be protected to afford the required fire-resistance rating of the wall or floor supported in buildings of other than Type IIB, IIIB or VB construction. Smoke-barrier walls used to separate smoke compartments shall comply with Section 709.4.1. Smoke-barrier walls used to enclose areas of refuge in accordance with Section 1009.6.4 or to enclose elevator lobbies in accordance with Section 405.4.3, 3007.6.2, or 3008.6.2 shall comply with Section 709.4.2. Exception: Smoke-barrier walls are not required in interstitial spaces where such spaces are designed and constructed with ceil- ings or exterior walls that provide resistance to the passage of fire and smoke equivalent to that provided by the smoke-barrier walls. | drafted:general.bc_709_4_smoke_barrier_continuity |
| 709.4.1 | Smoke-barrier assemblies separating smoke compartments | flag | draft | Smoke barriers dividing smoke compartments form a membrane continuous from an outside wall or smoke barrier to an outside wall or another smoke barrier, and to the floors and roof: a person's check (general.bc_709_4_1_smoke_compartment_barriers). | 709.4.1 Smoke-barrier assemblies separating smoke compartments. Smoke-barrier assemblies used to separate smoke compartments shall form an effective membrane enclosure that is continuous from an outside wall or smoke barrier wall to an outside wall or another smoke barrier wall and to the horizontal assemblies. | drafted:general.bc_709_4_1_smoke_compartment_barriers |
| 709.4.2 | Smoke-barrier walls enclosing areas of refuge or elevator lobbies | flag | draft | Smoke barrier walls enclosing an area of refuge (1009.6.4) or an elevator lobby (405.4.3, 3007.6.2, 3008.6.2) end at a fire barrier rated at least 1 hour, another smoke barrier or an outside wall; a smoke and draft control door (716.2.2.1.1) isn't required at each elevator hoistway door protected by a lobby, at each exit door into a protected lobby, or at each exit doorway between an area of refuge and the exit enclosure: a person's check (general.bc_709_4_2_refuge_lobby_smoke_barriers). | 709.4.2 Smoke-barrier walls enclosing areas of refuge or elevator lobbies. Smoke-barrier walls used to enclose areas of refuge in accordance with Section 1009.6.4, or to enclose elevator lobbies in accordance with Section 405.4.3, 3007.6.2, or 3008.6.2, shall form an effective membrane enclosure that terminates at a fire barrier wall having a fire resistance rating not less than 1 hour, another smoke barrier wall or an outside wall. A smoke and draft control door assembly as specified in Section 716.2.2.1.1 shall not be required at each elevator hoistway door where protected by an elevator lobby, at each exit door opening into a protected lobby or at each exit doorway between an area of refuge and the exit enclosure. | drafted:general.bc_709_4_2_refuge_lobby_smoke_barriers |
| 709.5 | Openings | flag | draft | Openings in a smoke barrier are protected per Section 716 (Table 716.1(2): a door at least 20 minutes; Table 716.1(3): a fire window 3/4 hour, OH-45 or W-60); except, in Group I-1 Condition 2, Group I-2 and ambulatory care facilities, a pair of opposite-swinging doors across a corridor per 709.5.1, close fitting, with no center mullion, undercuts over 3/4 inch, louvers or grilles, with head and jamb stops and astragals or rabbets at the meeting edges (positive latching not required; protective plates need no label), and special-purpose horizontal sliding, accordion or folding doors per 1010.3.3 protected per 716: a person's check (general.bc_709_5_smoke_barrier_openings). | 709.5 Openings. Openings in a smoke barrier shall be protected in accordance with Section 716. Exceptions: 1. In Group I-1, Condition 2, Group I-2 and ambulatory care facilities, where a pair of opposite-swinging doors are installed across a corridor in accordance with Section 709.5.1, the doors shall not be required to be protected in accordance with Section 716. The doors shall be close fitting within operational tolerances, and shall not have a center mullion or under- cuts in excess of 3/4 inch (19.1 mm), louvers or grilles. The doors shall have head and jamb stops, and astragals or rabbets at meeting edges. Positive latching devices are not required. Factory-applied or field-applied protective plates are not required to be labeled. 2. In Group I-1, Condition 2, Group I-2 and ambulatory care facilities, special purpose horizontal sliding, accordion or fold- ing doors installed in accordance with Section 1010.3.3 and protected in accordance with Section 716. | drafted:general.bc_709_5_smoke_barrier_openings |
| 709.5.1 | Group I-2 and ambulatory care facilities | flag | draft | In Group I-2 and ambulatory care facilities (and Group I-1 Condition 2, through 709.5 exception 1), smoke barrier doors across a corridor with hold-open devices close automatically per 716.2.6.6, and have a vision panel of fire-protection-rated glazing in fire-protection-rated frames no larger than tested: a person's check (general.bc_709_5_1_i2_smoke_barrier_doors). | 709.5.1 Group I-2 and ambulatory care facilities. In Group I-2 and ambulatory care facilities, where doors protecting openings in smoke barriers are installed across a corridor and have hold-open devices, the doors shall be automatic-closing in accordance with Section 716.2.6.6. Such doors shall have a vision panel with fire-protection-rated glazing materials in fire-protection-rated frames, the area of which shall not exceed that tested. | drafted:general.bc_709_5_1_i2_smoke_barrier_doors |
| 709.6 | Penetrations | flag | draft | Penetrations of a smoke barrier meet Section 714: a person's check (general.bc_709_6_smoke_barrier_penetrations). | 709.6 Penetrations. Penetrations of smoke barriers shall comply with Section 714. | drafted:general.bc_709_6_smoke_barrier_penetrations |
| 709.7 | Joints | flag | draft | Joints in or between smoke barriers meet Section 715: a person's check (general.bc_709_7_smoke_barrier_joints). | 709.7 Joints. Joints made in or between smoke barriers shall comply with Section 715. | drafted:general.bc_709_7_smoke_barrier_joints |
| 709.8 | Ducts and air transfer openings | flag | draft | Ducts and air transfer openings through a smoke barrier meet Section 717: a person's check (general.bc_709_8_smoke_barrier_ducts). | 709.8 Ducts and air transfer openings. Penetrations in a smoke barrier by ducts and air transfer openings shall comply with Section 717. | drafted:general.bc_709_8_smoke_barrier_ducts |
| 710.3 | Fire-resistance rating | flag | draft | A smoke partition (a Group I-2 corridor's walls, 407.3; an elevator lobby's, 3006.3) needs no fire-resistance rating unless another section requires one: a person's check (general.bc_710_3_smoke_partition_rating). | 710.3 Fire-resistance rating. Unless required elsewhere in the code, smoke partitions are not required to have a fire-resistance rating. | drafted:general.bc_710_3_smoke_partition_rating |
| 710.4 | Continuity | flag | draft | A smoke partition runs from the foundation or floor below to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above, or to the underside of a ceiling whose membrane limits the passage of smoke; in Group I-2, a lay-in ceiling counts where its tiles weigh at least 1 pound per square foot and the HVAC system is fully ducted per 603 of the Mechanical Code of New York State: a person's check (general.bc_710_4_smoke_partition_continuity). | 710.4 Continuity. Smoke partitions shall extend from the top of the foundation or floor below to the underside of the floor or roof sheathing, deck or slab above or to the underside of the ceiling above where the ceiling membrane is constructed to limit the transfer of smoke. Exception: In Group I-2, a lay-in ceiling system shall be considered capable of limiting the transfer of smoke where the ceiling tiles weigh not less than 1 pound per square foot (4.882 kg/m2) and where the HVAC system is fully ducted in accordance with Section 603 of the Mechanical Code of New York State. | drafted:general.bc_710_4_smoke_partition_continuity |
| 710.4.1 | Smoke partition walls enclosing elevator lobbies | flag | draft | Smoke partitions enclosing an elevator lobby per 3006.3 form an enclosure ending at a fire barrier rated at least 1 hour, another smoke partition or an outside wall: a person's check (general.bc_710_4_1_elevator_lobby_smoke_partitions). | 710.4.1 Smoke partition walls enclosing elevator lobbies. Smoke partition walls used to enclose elevator lobbies in accor- dance with Section 3006.3 shall form an enclosure that terminates at a fire barrier having a fire-resistance rating of not less than 1 hour, another smoke partition or an outside wall. | drafted:general.bc_710_4_1_elevator_lobby_smoke_partitions |
| 710.5.1 | Windows | flag | draft | Windows in a smoke partition are sealed against the free passage of smoke, or close automatically on detecting smoke: a person's check (general.bc_710_5_1_smoke_partition_windows). | 710.5.1 Windows. Windows in smoke partitions shall be sealed to resist the free passage of smoke or be automatic-closing upon detection of smoke. | drafted:general.bc_710_5_1_smoke_partition_windows |
| 710.5.2.1 | Louvers | flag | draft | Doors in a smoke partition have no louvers, except where 407.3.1.1 permits them (Group I-2): a person's check (general.bc_710_5_2_1_smoke_partition_door_louvers). | 710.5.2.1 Louvers. Doors in smoke partitions shall not include louvers. Exception: Where permitted in accordance with Section 407.3.1.1. | drafted:general.bc_710_5_2_1_smoke_partition_door_louvers |
| 710.5.2.2 | Smoke and draft control doors | flag | draft | Where another section requires it, a door in a smoke partition is a smoke and draft control door tested per UL 1784, leaking at most 3.0 cubic feet per minute per square foot of opening at 0.10 inch of water in both the ambient and elevated temperature tests, installed per NFPA 105: a person's check (general.bc_710_5_2_2_smoke_partition_draft_control_doors). | 710.5.2.2 Smoke and draft control doors. Where required elsewhere in the code, doors in smoke partitions shall meet the requirements for a smoke and draft control door assembly tested in accordance with UL 1784. The air leakage rate of the door assembly shall not exceed 3.0 cubic feet per minute per square foot [0.015424 m[fn3]/(s × m[fn2])] of door opening at 0.10 inch of water (25 Pa) for both the ambient temperature test and the elevated temperature exposure test. Installation of smoke doors shall be in accordance with NFPA 105. | drafted:general.bc_710_5_2_2_smoke_partition_draft_control_doors |
| 710.5.2.3 | Self- or automatic-closing doors | flag | draft | Where another section requires it, a door in a smoke partition is self-closing or closes automatically on smoke detection per 716.2.6.6: a person's check (general.bc_710_5_2_3_smoke_partition_self_closing_doors). | 710.5.2.3 Self- or automatic-closing doors. Where required elsewhere in the code, doors in smoke partitions shall be self- or automatic-closing by smoke detection in accordance with Section 716.2.6.6. | drafted:general.bc_710_5_2_3_smoke_partition_self_closing_doors |
| 710.5.3 | Pass-through openings in Group I-2, Condition 2 | flag | draft | In Group I-2 Condition 2, a pass-through opening in a smoke partition is allowed only where its smoke compartment holds no patient care suite or sleeping room, it is in a wall, door or vision panel not required to be rated, its top is at most 48 inches above the floor, and all such openings in one room total at most 80 square inches: whether a building is Condition 2, and its pass-through openings, aren't in the model, so a person's check.: a person's check (general.bc_710_5_3_i2_pass_through_openings). | 710.5.3 Pass-through openings in Group I-2, Condition 2. Where pass-through openings are provided in smoke partitions in Group I-2, Condition 2 occupancies, such openings shall comply with the following: 1. The smoke compartment in which the pass-through openings occur does not contain a patient care suite or sleeping room. 2. Pass-through openings are installed in a wall, door or vision panel that is not required to have a fire-resistance rating. 3. The top of the pass-through opening is located a maximum of 48 inches (1219 mm) above the floor. 4. The aggregate area of all such pass-through openings within a single room shall not exceed 80 square inches (0.05 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_710_5_3_i2_pass_through_openings |
| 710.6 | Penetrations | flag | draft | The space around anything passing through a smoke partition is filled with an approved material to limit the free passage of smoke: a person's check (general.bc_710_6_smoke_partition_penetrations). | 710.6 Penetrations. The space around penetrating items shall be filled with an approved material to limit the free passage of smoke. | drafted:general.bc_710_6_smoke_partition_penetrations |
| 710.7 | Joints | flag | draft | Joints in a smoke partition are filled with an approved material to limit the free passage of smoke: a person's check (general.bc_710_7_smoke_partition_joints). | 710.7 Joints. Joints shall be filled with an approved material to limit the free passage of smoke. | drafted:general.bc_710_7_smoke_partition_joints |
| 710.8 | Ducts and air transfer openings | flag | draft | The space around a duct through a smoke partition is filled with an approved material against smoke, and an air transfer opening in it has a smoke damper per 717.3.2.2, or, where a damper would interfere with a required smoke control system per 909, approved other protection: a person's check (general.bc_710_8_smoke_partition_ducts). | 710.8 Ducts and air transfer openings. The space around a duct penetrating a smoke partition shall be filled with an approved material to limit the free passage of smoke. Air transfer openings in smoke partitions shall be provided with a smoke damper comply- ing with Section 717.3.2.2. Exception: Where the installation of a smoke damper will interfere with the operation of a required smoke control system in accordance with Section 909, approved alternative protection shall be utilized. | drafted:general.bc_710_8_smoke_partition_ducts |
| 711.2.2 | Continuity | flag | draft | A horizontal assembly (a rated floor or roof, the top or bottom of an exit enclosure among them) is continuous, with no vertical openings but those this section and Section 712 permit: a person's check (general.bc_711_2_2_horizontal_assembly_continuity). | 711.2.2 Continuity. Assemblies shall be continuous without vertical openings, except as permitted by this section and Section 712. | drafted:general.bc_711_2_2_horizontal_assembly_continuity |
| 711.2.3 | Supporting construction | flag | draft | The construction supporting a horizontal assembly is protected to its required rating; except, in Types IIB, IIIB and VB, at incidental use separations rated at most 1 hour (Table 509.1), dwelling and sleeping unit separations (420.3), and smoke barriers per 709: a person's check (general.bc_711_2_3_horizontal_assembly_support). | 711.2.3 Supporting construction. The supporting construction shall be protected to afford the required fire-resistance rating of the horizontal assembly supported. Exception: In buildings of Type IIB, IIIB or VB construction, the construction supporting the horizontal assembly is not required to be fire-resistance rated at the following: 1. Horizontal assemblies at the separations of incidental uses as specified by Table 509.1 provided that the required fire- resistance rating does not exceed 1 hour. 2. Horizontal assemblies at the separations of dwelling units and sleeping units as required by Section 420.3. 3. Horizontal assemblies at smoke barriers constructed in accordance with Section 709. | drafted:general.bc_711_2_3_horizontal_assembly_support |
| 711.2.4 | Fire-resistance rating | flag | draft | A horizontal assembly is rated as 711.2.4.1 to 711.2.4.6 ask, and at least as the building's type of construction asks (Table 601): a person's check (general.bc_711_2_4_horizontal_assembly_rating). | 711.2.4 Fire-resistance rating. The fire-resistance rating of horizontal assemblies shall comply with Sections 711.2.4.1 through 711.2.4.6 but shall be not less than that required by the building type of construction. | drafted:general.bc_711_2_4_horizontal_assembly_rating |
| 711.2.4.4 | Separating smoke compartments | flag | draft | Where a horizontal assembly must be a smoke barrier (between smoke compartments), it meets Section 709: a person's check (general.bc_711_2_4_4_smoke_compartment_floors). | 711.2.4.4 Separating smoke compartments. Where the horizontal assembly is required to be a smoke barrier, the assembly shall comply with Section 709. | drafted:general.bc_711_2_4_4_smoke_compartment_floors |
| 711.2.4.6 | Other separations | flag | draft | Where another section requires a horizontal assembly (an exit stairway's or passageway's enclosure top or bottom, 1023.2 and 1024.3; a shaft's, 713), it is rated at least as that section asks: a person's check (general.bc_711_2_4_6_other_horizontal_separations). | 711.2.4.6 Other separations. Where a horizontal assembly is required by other sections of this code, the assembly shall have a fire-resistance rating of not less than that required by that section. | drafted:general.bc_711_2_4_6_other_horizontal_separations |
| 711.3.2 | Continuity | flag | draft | A floor or roof not required to be rated is continuous, with no vertical openings but those Section 712 permits (an exit access stairway per 1019, 712.1.12; a two-story opening, 712.1.9): a person's check (general.bc_711_3_2_unrated_floor_continuity). | 711.3.2 Continuity. Assemblies shall be continuous without vertical openings, except as permitted by Section 712. | drafted:general.bc_711_3_2_unrated_floor_continuity |
| 712.1 | General | flag | draft | Each vertical opening (a stair, ramp, elevator, duct or other opening through a floor) is protected by one of the methods of 712.1.1 to 712.1.16: in a shaft enclosure per 713 (712.1.1), a two-story opening (712.1.9), an exit access stairway or ramp per 1019 (712.1.12), or another the section lists (escalators, atriums, parking garages, mezzanines, and the like, 712.1.2 to 712.1.8, 712.1.10, 712.1.11, 712.1.13 to 712.1.16): a person's check (general.bc_712_1_vertical_openings). | 712.1 General. Each vertical opening shall comply in accordance with one of the protection methods in Sections 712.1.1 through 712.1.16. | drafted:general.bc_712_1_vertical_openings |
| 712.1.9 | Two-story openings | flag | draft | In other than Groups I-2 and I-3, a vertical opening that is none of 712.1's other cases (an exit access stairway may use it, 1019.3) is permitted only where it connects at most two stories, penetrates no horizontal assembly separating fire areas and no smoke barrier separating smoke compartments, isn't concealed in a wall or floor/ceiling, isn't open to a corridor in Group I or R, isn't open to a corridor on an unsprinklered floor, and is separated from floor and air transfer openings serving other floors by construction as for the required shaft enclosures: a person's check (general.bc_712_1_9_two_story_openings). | 712.1.9 Two-story openings. In other than Groups I-2 and I-3, a vertical opening that is not used as one of the applications spec- ified in this section shall be permitted if the opening complies with all of the following items: 1. Does not connect more than two stories. 2. Does not penetrate a horizontal assembly that separates fire areas or smoke barriers that separate smoke compartments. 3. Is not concealed within the construction of a wall or a floor/ceiling assembly. 4. Is not open to a corridor in Group I and R occupancies. 5. Is not open to a corridor on nonsprinklered floors. 6. Is separated from floor openings and air transfer openings serving other floors by construction conforming to required shaft enclosures. | drafted:general.bc_712_1_9_two_story_openings |
| 713.1 | General | flag | draft | Section 713 applies to shafts through floors and roofs, and interior exit stairways and ramps are enclosed per Section 1023: a requirement no other Chapter 7 flag carries, a person's check (general.bc_713_1_shafts_and_exit_stairways). | 713.1 General. The provisions of this section shall apply to shafts required to protect openings and penetrations through floor/ceil- ing and roof/ceiling assemblies. Interior exit stairways and ramps shall be enclosed in accordance with Section 1023. | drafted:general.bc_713_1_shafts_and_exit_stairways |
| 713.2 | Construction | flag | draft | Shaft enclosures are built as fire barriers (707) or horizontal assemblies (711): not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_2_shaft_construction). | 713.2 Construction. Shaft enclosures shall be constructed as fire barriers in accordance with Section 707 or horizontal assemblies in accordance with Section 711, or both. | drafted:general.bc_713_2_shaft_construction |
| 713.4 | Fire-resistance rating | flag | draft | A shaft enclosure is rated at least 2 hours where it connects four stories or more, at least 1 hour where fewer, and at least the floor it passes through, but need not exceed 2 hours (403.2.1.2's high-rise reduction aside): its rating and the stories it connects aren't in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_4_shaft_rating). | 713.4 Fire-resistance rating. Shaft enclosures shall have a fire-resistance rating of not less than 2 hours where connecting four stories or more, and not less than 1 hour where connecting less than four stories. The number of stories connected by the shaft enclo- sure shall include any basements but not any mezzanines. Shaft enclosures shall have a fire-resistance rating not less than the floor assembly penetrated, but need not exceed 2 hours. Shaft enclosures shall meet the requirements of Section 703.2.1.1. Exception: Shafts having a reduced fire-resistance rating in high-rise buildings in accordance with Section 403.2.1.2. | drafted:general.bc_713_4_shaft_rating |
| 713.5 | Continuity | flag | draft | Shaft enclosures are continuous per 707.5 or 711.2.2: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_5_shaft_continuity). | 713.5 Continuity. Shaft enclosures shall be constructed as fire barriers in accordance with Section 707 or horizontal assemblies constructed in accordance with Section 711, or both, and shall have continuity in accordance with Section 707.5 for fire barriers or Section 711.2.2 for horizontal assemblies, as applicable. | drafted:general.bc_713_5_shaft_continuity |
| 713.6 | Exterior walls | flag | draft | An exterior wall that is part of a shaft enclosure follows Section 705 instead, with its exceptions: a person's check (general.bc_713_6_shaft_exterior_walls). | 713.6 Exterior walls. Where exterior walls serve as a part of a required shaft enclosure, such walls shall comply with the require- ments of Section 705 for exterior walls and the fire-resistance-rated enclosure requirements shall not apply. Exception: Exterior walls required to be fire-resistance rated in accordance with Section 1021.2 for exterior egress balconies, Section 1023.7 for interior exit stairways and ramps and Section 1027.6 for exterior exit stairways and ramps. | drafted:general.bc_713_6_shaft_exterior_walls |
| 713.7 | Openings | flag | draft | Openings in shaft enclosures are protected per Section 716 and their doors self- or automatic-closing: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_7_shaft_openings). | 713.7 Openings. Openings in a shaft enclosure shall be protected in accordance with Section 716 as required for fire barriers. Doors shall be self- or automatic-closing by smoke detection in accordance with Section 716.2.6.6. | drafted:general.bc_713_7_shaft_openings |
| 713.7.1 | Prohibited openings | flag | draft | No openings in a shaft enclosure but those its purpose needs: a person's check (general.bc_713_7_1_shaft_prohibited_openings). | 713.7.1 Prohibited openings. Openings other than those necessary for the purpose of the shaft shall not be permitted in shaft enclosures. | drafted:general.bc_713_7_1_shaft_prohibited_openings |
| 713.8 | Penetrations | flag | draft | Penetrations of shaft enclosures are protected per Section 714: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_8_shaft_penetrations). | 713.8 Penetrations. Penetrations in a shaft enclosure shall be protected in accordance with Section 714 as required for fire barriers or horizontal assemblies or both. Structural elements, such as beams or joists, where protected in accordance with Section 714 shall be permitted to penetrate a shaft enclosure. | drafted:general.bc_713_8_shaft_penetrations |
| 713.8.1 | Prohibited penetrations | flag | draft | No penetrations of a shaft enclosure but those its purpose needs, membrane penetrations on its outside per 714.4.2 aside: a person's check (general.bc_713_8_1_shaft_prohibited_penetrations). | 713.8.1 Prohibited penetrations. Penetrations other than those necessary for the purpose of the shaft shall not be permitted in shaft enclosures. Exception: Membrane penetrations shall be permitted on the outside of shaft enclosures. Such penetrations shall be protected in accordance with Section 714.4.2. | drafted:general.bc_713_8_1_shaft_prohibited_penetrations |
| 713.9 | Joints | flag | draft | Joints in shaft enclosures meet Section 715: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_9_shaft_joints). | 713.9 Joints. Joints in a shaft enclosure shall comply with Section 715. | drafted:general.bc_713_9_shaft_joints |
| 713.10 | Duct and air transfer openings | flag | draft | Ducts and air transfer openings through shaft enclosures meet Section 717: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_10_shaft_ducts). | 713.10 Duct and air transfer openings. Penetrations of a shaft enclosure by ducts and air transfer openings shall comply with Section 717. | drafted:general.bc_713_10_shaft_ducts |
| 713.11 | Enclosure at the bottom | flag | draft | A shaft that doesn't reach the bottom of the building is closed at its foot in one of three ways, with exceptions: a person's check (general.bc_713_11_shaft_bottom). | 713.11 Enclosure at the bottom. Shafts that do not extend to the bottom of the building or structure shall comply with one of the following: | drafted:general.bc_713_11_shaft_bottom |
| 713.12 | Enclosure at top | flag | draft | The top of a shaft enclosure reaches the roof, is closed below it at the topmost floor's rating, or rises through it per 1511: a person's check (general.bc_713_12_shaft_top). | 713.12 Enclosure at top. The top of shaft enclosures shall comply with one of the following: 1. Extend to the underside of the roof sheathing, deck or slab of the building, and the roof assembly shall comply with the requirements for the type of construction as specified in Table 601. 2. Terminate below the roof assembly and be enclosed at the top with construction of the same fire-resistance rating as the topmost floor penetrated by the shaft, but not less than the fire-resistance rating required for the shaft enclosure. 3. Extend past the roof assembly and comply with the requirements of Section 1511. | drafted:general.bc_713_12_shaft_top |
| 713.14 | Elevator, dumbwaiter and other hoistways | flag | draft | Hoistways meet Section 712, and where enclosed are shaft enclosures per 713 and Chapter 30: the elevators aren't in the model, a person's check (general.bc_713_14_hoistways). | 713.14 Elevator, dumbwaiter and other hoistways. A hoistway for elevators, dumbwaiters and other vertical devices shall comply with Section 712. Where the hoistway is required to be enclosed, it shall be constructed as a shaft enclosure in accordance with Section 713 and Chapter 30. | drafted:general.bc_713_14_hoistways |
| 714.2 | Installation | flag | draft | A listed penetration firestop system is installed per the manufacturer's instructions and its listing: a person's check (general.bc_714_2_penetration_firestop_installation). | 714.2 Installation. A listed penetration firestop system shall be installed in accordance with the manufacturer’s installation instruc- tions and the listing criteria. | drafted:general.bc_714_2_penetration_firestop_installation |
| 714.3 | Sleeves | flag | draft | A sleeve through a rated assembly is securely fastened and installed per its maker's instructions (and the listing, for a listed system); the space inside and around it is protected per Section 714, and insulation or a covering on the item passes through only where tested as part of the assembly: a person's check (general.bc_714_3_sleeves). | 714.3 Sleeves. Where sleeves are used, they shall be securely fastened to the assembly penetrated and installed in accordance with the sleeve manufacturer’s installation instructions. Where listed systems are used, the sleeve shall be installed in accordance with the listing criteria for the system. The space between the item contained in the sleeve and the sleeve itself and any space between the sleeve and the assembly penetrated shall be protected in accordance with this section. Insulation and coverings on or in the penetrating item shall not penetrate the assembly unless the specific material used has been tested as part of the assembly in accor- dance with this section. | drafted:general.bc_714_3_sleeves |
| 714.4 | Fire-resistance-rated walls | flag | draft | Penetrations into or through fire walls, fire barriers, smoke barrier walls and fire partitions (the walls of exit enclosures and rated corridors among them) meet 714.4.1 to 714.4.3, and those in smoke barrier walls also 714.5.4 (air leakage): a person's check (general.bc_714_4_rated_wall_penetrations). | 714.4 Fire-resistance-rated walls. Penetrations into or through fire walls, fire barriers, smoke barrier walls and fire partitions shall comply with Sections 714.4.1 through 714.4.3. Penetrations in smoke barrier walls shall also comply with Section 714.5.4. | drafted:general.bc_714_4_rated_wall_penetrations |
| 714.4.1 | Through penetrations | flag | draft | A through penetration of a rated wall meets 714.4.1.1 or 714.4.1.2; except a steel, ferrous or copper pipe, tube or conduit, whose annular space may be filled with concrete, grout or mortar the full thickness of a concrete or masonry wall or the thickness that keeps its rating (an item at most 6 inches nominal diameter through an opening at most 144 square inches), or with a material that stops flame and hot gases for the wall's rating under ASTM E119 or UL 263 fire conditions at a positive pressure differential of at least 0.01 inch of water: a person's check (general.bc_714_4_1_through_penetrations). | 714.4.1 Through penetrations. Through penetrations of fire-resistance-rated walls shall comply with Section 714.4.1.1 or 714.4.1.2. Exception: Where the penetrating items are steel, ferrous or copper pipes, tubes or conduits, the annular space between the penetrating item and the fire-resistance-rated wall is permitted to be protected by either of the following measures: 1. In concrete or masonry walls where the penetrating item is a maximum 6-inch (152 mm) nominal diameter and the area of the opening through the wall does not exceed 144 square inches (0.0929 m[fn2]), concrete, grout or mortar is permitted where installed the full thickness of the wall or the thickness required to maintain the fire-resistance rating. 2. The material used to fill the annular space shall prevent the passage of flame and hot gases sufficient to ignite cotton waste when subjected to ASTM E119 or UL 263 time-temperature fire conditions under a minimum positive pressure differential of 0.01 inch of water (2.49 Pa) at the location of the penetration for the time period equivalent to the fire- resistance rating of the construction penetrated. | drafted:general.bc_714_4_1_through_penetrations |
| 714.4.1.1 | Fire-resistance-rated assemblies | flag | draft | Through penetrations of a rated wall, where not protected per 714.4.1.2 (a firestop system), are protected by systems installed as tested in the approved rated assembly: a person's check (general.bc_714_4_1_1_tested_assembly_penetrations). | 714.4.1.1 Fire-resistance-rated assemblies. Through penetrations shall be protected using systems installed as tested in the approved fire-resistance-rated assembly. | drafted:general.bc_714_4_1_1_tested_assembly_penetrations |
| 714.4.1.2 | Through-penetration firestop system | flag | draft | Through penetrations of a rated wall, where not protected as tested in the assembly per 714.4.1.1, are protected by an approved through-penetration firestop system installed as tested per ASTM E814 or UL 1479 at a positive pressure differential of at least 0.01 inch of water, with an F rating at least the wall's required rating: a person's check (general.bc_714_4_1_2_through_penetration_firestop). | 714.4.1.2 Through-penetration firestop system. Through penetrations shall be protected by an approved penetration firestop system installed as tested in accordance with ASTM E814 or UL 1479, with a minimum positive pressure differential of 0.01 inch of water (2.49 Pa) and shall have an F rating of not less than the required fire-resistance rating of the wall penetrated. | drafted:general.bc_714_4_1_2_through_penetration_firestop |
| 714.4.2 | Membrane penetrations | flag | draft | A membrane penetration of a rated wall meets 714.4.1, and recessed fixtures don't reduce the wall's rating; except steel electrical boxes in walls rated at most 2 hours, each at most 16 square inches, at most 100 square inches of openings in any 100 square feet of wall, an annular space at most 1/8 inch, boxes on opposite sides at least 24 inches apart where the stud cavities don't communicate (or the wall cavity's depth where filled with cellulose loose-fill, rockwool or slag wool, or solid fireblocking per 718.2.1, or both boxes with listed putty pads, or other listed materials and methods); listed electrical boxes of any material tested for use in fire-resistance-rated assemblies, installed per their listing (1/8 inch unless listed otherwise; their listed separation, solid fireblocking per 718.2.1, both boxes with listed putty pads, or other listed materials and methods); electrical boxes listed as part of a wall opening protective material system for use in fire-resistance-rated assemblies, installed per their listing; other boxes and their annular space protected by an approved membrane penetration firestop system installed as tested per ASTM E814 or UL 1479 at a positive pressure differential of at least 0.01 inch of water, with F and T ratings at least the wall's, installed per their listing; the space around a sprinkler covered by a metal escutcheon plate; and larger or more steel boxes in walls rated at most 2 hours with listed putty pads or other listed materials and methods, installed per the listing: a person's check (general.bc_714_4_2_membrane_penetrations). | 714.4.2 Membrane penetrations. Membrane penetrations shall comply with Section 714.4.1. Where walls or partitions are required to have a fire-resistance rating, recessed fixtures shall be installed such that the required fire resistance will not be reduced. Exceptions: 1. Membrane penetrations of maximum 2-hour fire-resistance-rated walls and partitions by steel electrical boxes that do not exceed 16 square inches (0.0 103 m[fn2]) in area, provided that the aggregate area of the openings through the membrane does not exceed 100 square inches (0.0645 m[fn2]) in any 100 square feet (9.29 m[fn2]) of wall area. The annular space between the wall membrane and the box shall not exceed 1/8 inch (3.2 mm). Such boxes on opposite sides of the wall or partition shall be separated by one of the following: 1.1. By a horizontal distance of not less than 24 inches (610 mm) where the wall or partition is constructed with individual noncommunicating stud cavities. 1.2. By a horizontal distance of not less than the depth of the wall cavity where the wall cavity is filled with cellu- lose loose-fill, rockwool or slag mineral wool insulation. 1.3. By solid fireblocking in accordance with Section 718.2.1. 1.4. By protecting both outlet boxes with listed putty pads. 1.5. By other listed materials and methods. 2. Membrane penetrations by listed electrical boxes of any material, provided that such boxes have been tested for use in fire-resistance-rated assemblies and are installed in accordance with the instructions included in the listing. The annular space between the wall membrane and the box shall not exceed 1/8 inch (3.2 mm) unless listed otherwise. Such boxes on opposite sides of the wall or partition shall be separated by one of the following: 2.1. By the horizontal distance specified in the listing of the electrical boxes. 2.2. By solid fireblocking in accordance with Section 718.2.1. 2.3. By protecting both boxes with listed putty pads. 2.4. By other listed materials and methods. | drafted:general.bc_714_4_2_membrane_penetrations |
| 714.4.3 | Dissimilar materials | flag | draft | A noncombustible penetrating item doesn't connect to combustible items beyond the firestopping unless the wall's fire resistance is shown to be kept: a person's check (general.bc_714_4_3_dissimilar_materials). | 714.4.3 Dissimilar materials. Noncombustible penetrating items shall not connect to combustible items beyond the point of firestopping unless it can be demonstrated that the fire-resistance integrity of the wall is maintained. | drafted:general.bc_714_4_3_dissimilar_materials |
| 714.5 | Horizontal assemblies | flag | draft | Penetrations of a rated floor, floor/ceiling assembly or a roof/ceiling assembly's ceiling membrane (the top or bottom of an exit stairway or passageway enclosure among them, 1023.5, 1024.6) that 712.1 doesn't require in a shaft meet 714.5.1 to 714.5.4: a person's check (general.bc_714_5_horizontal_assembly_penetrations). | 714.5 Horizontal assemblies. Penetrations of a fire-resistance-rated floor, floor/ceiling assembly or the ceiling membrane of a roof/ceiling assembly not required to be enclosed in a shaft by Section 712.1 shall be protected in accordance with Sections 714.5.1 through 714.5.4. | drafted:general.bc_714_5_horizontal_assembly_penetrations |
| 714.5.1 | Through penetrations | flag | draft | A through penetration of a rated floor meets 714.5.1.1 or 714.5.1.2; except steel, ferrous or copper conduits, pipes, tubes or vents or concrete or masonry items through a single rated floor with the annular space stopping flame and hot gases for the floor's rating under ASTM E119 or UL 263 fire conditions at a positive pressure differential of at least 0.01 inch of water (items at most 6 inches nominal diameter may pass more than one floor where the openings total at most 144 square inches in any 100 square feet of floor); steel, ferrous or copper conduits, pipes, tubes or vents at most 6 inches through a single concrete floor with concrete, grout or mortar the full thickness or the thickness that keeps its rating (more than one floor where each opening is at most 144 square inches); listed electrical boxes of any material tested for use in fire-resistance-rated assemblies, installed per their listing; and concrete floors or ramps of parking garages per 406.5 and 406.6 with parking above and below: a person's check (general.bc_714_5_1_floor_through_penetrations). | 714.5.1 Through penetrations. Through penetrations of horizontal assemblies shall comply with Section 714.5.1.1 or 714.5.1.2. Exceptions: 1. Penetrations by steel, ferrous or copper conduits, pipes, tubes or vents or concrete or masonry items through a single fire-resistance-rated floor assembly where the annular space is protected with materials that prevent the passage of flame and hot gases sufficient to ignite cotton waste when subjected to ASTM E119 or UL 263 time-temperature fire conditions under a minimum positive pressure differential of 0.01 inch of water (2.49 Pa) at the location of the pene- tration for the time period equivalent to the fire-resistance rating of the construction penetrated. Penetrating items with a maximum 6-inch (152 mm) nominal diameter shall not be limited to the penetration of a single fire-resistance- rated floor assembly, provided that the aggregate area of the openings through the assembly does not exceed 144 square inches (92 900 mm [fn2]) in any 100 square feet (9.3 m[fn2]) of floor area. 2. Penetrations in a single concrete floor by steel, ferrous or copper conduits, pipes, tubes or vents with a maximum 6- inch (152 mm) nominal diameter, provided that the concrete, grout or mortar is installed the full thickness of the floor or the thickness required to maintain the fire-resistance rating. The penetrating items shall not be limited to the pene- tration of a single concrete floor, provided that the area of the opening through each floor does not exceed 144 square inches (92 900 mm[fn2]). 3. Penetrations by listed electrical boxes of any material, provided that such boxes have been tested for use in fire-resis- tance-rated assemblies and installed in accordance with the instructions included in the listing. 4. Penetrations of concrete floors or ramps within parking garages or structures constructed in accordance with Sections 406.5 and 406.6 where the areas above and below the penetrations are parking areas. | drafted:general.bc_714_5_1_floor_through_penetrations |
| 714.5.1.1 | Fire-resistance-rated assemblies | flag | draft | Through penetrations of a rated floor, where not protected per 714.5.1.2 (a firestop system), are protected by systems installed as tested in the approved rated assembly: a person's check (general.bc_714_5_1_1_floor_tested_assembly_penetrations). | 714.5.1.1 Fire-resistance-rated assemblies. Through penetrations shall be protected using systems installed as tested in the approved fire-resistance-rated assembly. | drafted:general.bc_714_5_1_1_floor_tested_assembly_penetrations |
| 714.5.1.2 | Through-penetration firestop system | flag | draft | Through penetrations of a rated floor, where not protected as tested in the assembly per 714.5.1.1, are protected by an approved through-penetration firestop system installed and tested per ASTM E814 or UL 1479 at a positive pressure differential of at least 0.01 inch of water, with an F rating and a T rating of at least 1 hour and at least the floor's required rating; no T rating is needed for floor penetrations within a wall cavity above or below, floor, tub or shower drains within a horizontal assembly's concealed space, metal conduit or tubing at most 4 inches nominal diameter penetrating directly into metal-enclosed electrical power switchgear, or steel, ferrous or copper conduits, pipes, tubes or vents at most 6 inches through a single concrete floor (more than one floor where each opening is at most 144 square inches): a person's check (general.bc_714_5_1_2_floor_firestop_systems). | 714.5.1.2 Through-penetration firestop system. Through penetrations shall be protected by an approved through-penetra- tion firestop system installed and tested in accordance with ASTM E814 or UL 1479, with a minimum positive pressure differential of 0.01 inch of water (2.49 Pa). The system shall have an F rating/T rating of not less than 1 hour but not less than the required rating of the floor penetrated. Exceptions: 1. Floor penetrations contained and located within the cavity of a wall above the floor or below the floor do not require a T rating. 2. Floor penetrations by floor drains, tub drains or shower drains contained and located within the concealed space of a horizontal assembly do not require a T rating. 3. Floor penetrations of maximum 4-inch (102 mm) nominal diameter metal conduit or tubing penetrating directly into metal-enclosed electrical power switchgear do not require a T rating. 4. Penetrations in a single concrete floor by steel, ferrous or copper conduits, pipes, tubes or vents with a maximum 6-inch (152 mm) nominal diameter do not require a T rating. These penetrating items shall not be limited to the penetration of a single concrete floor, provided that the area of the opening through each floor does not exceed 144 square inches (92,900 mm[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_714_5_1_2_floor_firestop_systems |
| 714.5.2 | Membrane penetrations | flag | draft | A membrane penetration of a horizontal assembly meets 714.5.1.1 or 714.5.1.2, and recessed fixtures don't reduce a rated floor/ceiling's rating; except steel, ferrous or copper conduits, pipes, tubes or vents or concrete or masonry items with the annular space protected per 714.5.1 or against flame and the products of combustion, at most 100 square inches of openings in any 100 square feet of ceiling (in assemblies tested without penetrations); steel electrical boxes in ceilings of assemblies rated at most 2 hours, each at most 16 square inches, at most 100 square inches in any 100 square feet, an annular space at most 1/8 inch; electrical boxes listed as part of an opening protective material system for use in horizontal assemblies, installed per their listing; listed electrical boxes of any material tested for use in fire-resistance-rated assemblies, installed per their listing (1/8 inch unless listed otherwise); the space around a sprinkler covered by a metal escutcheon plate; noncombustible items cast into concrete that don't pass both faces; the double wood top plate of a wall sheathed with Type X gypsum board interrupting the ceiling of an assembly rated at most 2 hours, its penetrations protected per 714.5.1.1 or 714.5.1.2 and the ceiling tight to the plates; and ceiling membrane penetrations by listed luminaires, or luminaires protected with listed materials, tested for rated assemblies and installed per their listing: a person's check (general.bc_714_5_2_floor_membrane_penetrations). | 714.5.2 Membrane penetrations. Penetrations of membranes that are part of a horizontal assembly shall comply with Section 714.5.1.1 or 714.5.1.2. Where floor/ceiling assemblies are required to have a fire-resistance rating, recessed fixtures shall be installed such that the required fire resistance will not be reduced. | drafted:general.bc_714_5_2_floor_membrane_penetrations |
| 714.5.3 | Dissimilar materials | flag | draft | A noncombustible penetrating item doesn't connect to combustible materials beyond the firestopping unless the horizontal assembly's fire resistance is shown to be kept: a person's check (general.bc_714_5_3_floor_dissimilar_materials). | 714.5.3 Dissimilar materials. Noncombustible penetrating items shall not connect to combustible materials beyond the point of firestopping unless it can be demonstrated that the fire-resistance integrity of the horizontal assembly is maintained. | drafted:general.bc_714_5_3_floor_dissimilar_materials |
| 714.5.4 | Penetrations in smoke barriers | flag | draft | Penetrations in smoke barriers are protected by an approved through-penetration firestop system installed and tested per UL 1479 for air leakage, its L rating at 0.30 inch of water in both the ambient and elevated temperature tests at most 5.0 cfm per square foot of penetration opening for each system, and at most 50 cfm in all for any 100 square feet of wall or floor area: a person's check (general.bc_714_5_4_smoke_barrier_penetrations). | 714.5.4 Penetrations in smoke barriers. Penetrations in smoke barriers shall be protected by an approved through-penetration firestop system installed and tested in accordance with the requirements of UL 1479 for air leakage. The L rating of the system measured at 0.30 inch of water (74.7 Pa) in both the ambient temperature and elevated temperature tests shall not exceed either of the following: 1. 5.0 cfm per square foot (0.025 m[fn3]/ s × m [fn2]) of penetration opening for each through-penetration firestop system. 2. A total cumulative leakage of 50 cfm (0.024 m [fn3]/s) for any 100 square feet (9.3 m[fn2]) of wall area, or floor area. | drafted:general.bc_714_5_4_smoke_barrier_penetrations |
| 716.1 | General | flag | draft | Opening protectives other sections require meet Section 716 and are installed per NFPA 80: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_716_1_opening_protectives_general). | 716.1 General. Opening protectives required by other sections of this code shall comply with the provisions of this section and shall be installed in accordance with NFPA 80. | drafted:general.bc_716_1_opening_protectives_general |
| Table 716.1(2) | OPENING FIRE PROTECTION ASSEMBLIES, RATINGS AND MARKINGS | flag | draft | The fire protection rating of fire doors and shutters, and their vision panel and sidelight glazing, by the wall they are in (exit enclosures 1 1/2 or 1 hour, rated corridors and smoke barriers 1/3 hour, and so on): the doors' and walls' ratings aren't in the model, a person's check (general.bc_table_716_1_2__exit_enclosures, general.bc_table_716_1_2__corridors, general.bc_table_716_1_2__smoke_barriers, general.bc_table_716_1_2__other_assemblies). | TABLE 716.1(2)—OPENING FIRE PROTECTION ASSEMBLIES, RATINGS AND MARKINGS MINIMUM MINIMUM FIRE-RATED FIRE-RATED GLAZING FIRE DOOR SIDELIGHT/TRANSOM DOOR GLAZING MARKING SIDE- REQUIRED WALL AND FIRE ASSEMBLY RATING TYPE OF VISION MARKING LIGHT/TRANSOM PANEL ASSEMBLY RATING SHUTTER (hours) ASSEMBLY PANEL DOOR (hours) ASSEMBLY RATING SIZE[fna] VISION Fire Fire Fire Fire (hours) PANEL[fnb,] [fnc] protection resistance protection resistance See Not Not 4 3 D-H-W-240 4 W-240 Note a Permitted Permitted See Not Not 3 3d D-H-W-180 3 W-180 Fire walls and Note a Permitted Permitted fire barriers ≤100 sq. in. = having a required fire- 2 1[fn1]/ 100 sq. in. D-H-90 Not 2 Not W-120 resistance [fn2] >100 sq. in.= Permitted Permitted rating greater D-H-W-90 than 1 hour ≤100 sq. in. = D-H-90 Not Not 1[fn1]/ 1[fn1]/ 100 sq. in. 1[fn1]/ W-90 [fn2] [fn2] >100 sq. in.= Permitted [fn2] Permitted D-H-W-90 Each wall Single- of the wall double- assembly — wall rating assembly (hours)[fne] (hours)[fnf] Double See Not Not 4 3 3 D-H-W-180 3 W-180 fire walls Note a Permitted Permitted constructed in ≤ 100 sq. in. = accordance with NFPA 221 3 2 1[fn1]/ 100 sq. in. D-H-90 Not 2 Not W-120 [fn2] >100 sq. in.= Permitted Permitted D-H-W-90 ≤ 100 sq. in. = D-H-60 Not Not 2 1 1 100 sq. in. 1 W-60 > 100 sq. in. = Permitted Permitted D-H-W-60 | drafted:general.bc_table_716_1_2__exit_enclosures, drafted:general.bc_table_716_1_2__corridors, drafted:general.bc_table_716_1_2__smoke_barriers, drafted:general.bc_table_716_1_2__other_assemblies |
| Table 716.1(3) | FIRE WINDOW ASSEMBLY FIRE PROTECTION RATINGS | flag | draft | The fire protection rating of fire windows by the wall they are in: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_table_716_1_3__fire_windows). | TABLE 716.1(3)—FIRE WINDOW ASSEMBLY FIRE PROTECTION RATINGS REQUIRED WALL ASSEMBLY MINIMUM FIRE WINDOW FIRE-RATED TYPE OF WALL ASSEMBLY RATING (hours) ASSEMBLY RATING (hours) GLAZING MARKING Interior walls Fire walls All NP[fna] W-XXX[fnb] >1 NP[fna] W-XXX[fnb] Fire barriers 1 NP[fna] W-XXX[fnb] Atrium separations (Section 707.3.6), OH-45 or W-60 Incidental use areas (Section 707.3.7),[fnc] 1 3/4 Mixed occupancy separations (Section 707.3.9) 1 3/4 OH-45 or W-60 Fire partitions 0.5 1/3 OH-20 or W-30 Smoke barriers 1 3/4 OH-45 or W-60 >1 1 1/2 OH-90 or W-XXX[fnb] Exterior walls 1 3/4 OH-45 or W-60 0.5 1/3 OH-20 or W-30 Party wall All NP Not Applicable NP = Not Permitted. a.Not permitted except fire-resistance-rated glazing assemblies tested to ASTM E119 or UL 263, as specified in Section 716.1.2.3. b.XXX = The fire rating duration period in minutes, which shall be equal to the fire-resistance rating required for the wall assembly. c. Fire-protection-rated glazing is not permitted for fire barriers required by Section 1207 of the Fire Code of New York State to enclose energy storage systems. Fire-resistance- rated glazing assemblies tested to ASTM E119 or UL 263, as specified in Section 716.1.2.3, shall be permitted. | drafted:general.bc_table_716_1_3__fire_windows |
| 716.1.2.1 | Safety glazing | flag | draft | Fire-rated glazing also meets Chapter 24's safety glazing: a person's check (general.bc_716_1_2_1_safety_glazing). | 716.1.2.1 Safety glazing. Fire-protection-rated glazing and fire-resistance-rated glazing installed in fire door assemblies and fire window assemblies shall comply with the safety glazing requirements of Chapter 24 where applicable. | drafted:general.bc_716_1_2_1_safety_glazing |
| 716.1.2.2 | Marking fire-rated glazing assemblies | flag | draft | Fire-rated glazing is marked as the tables say: a person's check (general.bc_716_1_2_2_marking_glazing). | 716.1.2.2 Marking fire-rated glazing assemblies. Fire-rated glazing assemblies shall be marked in accordance with Tables 716.1(1), 716.1(2) and 716.1(3). | drafted:general.bc_716_1_2_2_marking_glazing |
| 716.1.2.2.1 | Fire-rated glazing identification | flag | draft | A fire-rated glazing label bears D, H, T and its rating in minutes as the tables require: a person's check (general.bc_716_1_2_2_1_glazing_identification). | 716.1.2.2.1 Fire-rated glazing identification. For fire-rated glazing, the label shall bear the identification required in Tables 716.1(1) and 716.1(2). “D” indicates that the glazing is permitted to be used in fire door assemblies and meets the fire protection requirements of NFPA 252, UL 10B or UL 10C. “H” indicates that the glazing meets the hose stream requirements of NFPA 252, UL 10B or UL 10C. “T” indicates that the glazing meets the temperature requirements of Section 716.2.2.3.1. The placeholder “XXX” represents the fire-rating period, in minutes. | drafted:general.bc_716_1_2_2_1_glazing_identification |
| 716.1.2.2.2 | Fire-protection-rated glazing identification | flag | draft | A fire-protection-rated glazing label bears OH and its rating in minutes: a person's check (general.bc_716_1_2_2_2_fire_protection_glazing_identification). | 716.1.2.2.2 Fire-protection-rated glazing identification. For fire-protection-rated glazing, the label shall bear the follow- ing identification required in Tables 716.1(1) and 716.1(3): “OH – XXX.” “OH” indicates that the glazing meets both the fire protection and the hose-stream requirements of NFPA 257 or UL 9 and is permitted to be used in fire window openings. The placeholder “XXX” represents the fire-rating period, in minutes. | drafted:general.bc_716_1_2_2_2_fire_protection_glazing_identification |
| 716.1.2.2.3 | Fire-resistance-rated glazing identification | flag | draft | A fire-resistance-rated glazing label bears what 703.4 and Table 716.1(1) require: a person's check (general.bc_716_1_2_2_3_fire_resistance_glazing_identification). | 716.1.2.2.3 Fire-resistance-rated glazing identification. For fire-resistance-rated glazing, the label shall bear the identifi- cation required in Section 703.4 and Table 716.1(1). | drafted:general.bc_716_1_2_2_3_fire_resistance_glazing_identification |
| 716.2.1 | Testing requirements | flag | draft | Fire doors and shutters meet the tests of 716.2.1.1 to 716.2.1.4 and Table 716.1(2)'s rating: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_1_fire_door_testing). | 716.2.1 Testing requirements. Approved fire door and fire shutter assemblies shall be constructed of any material or assembly of component materials that conforms to the test requirements of Sections 716.2.1.1 through 716.2.1.4 and the fire protection rating indicated in Table 716.1(2). Exceptions: 1. Labeled protective assemblies that conform to the requirements of this section or UL 10A, UL 14B and UL 14C for tin- clad fire door assemblies. 2. Floor fire door assemblies in accordance with Section 712.1.13.1. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_1_fire_door_testing |
| 716.2.1.1 | Side-hinged or pivoted swinging doors | flag | draft | Swinging fire doors are tested per NFPA 252 (positive pressure) or UL 10C: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_1_1_swinging_door_tests). | 716.2.1.1 Side-hinged or pivoted swinging doors. Fire door assemblies with side-hinged and pivoted swinging doors shall be tested in accordance with NFPA 252 or UL 10C. For tests conducted in accordance with NFPA 252, the fire test shall be conducted using the positive pressure method specified in the standard. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_1_1_swinging_door_tests |
| 716.2.1.2 | Other types of assemblies | flag | draft | Other fire doors and shutters are tested per NFPA 252 or UL 10B: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_1_2_other_door_tests). | 716.2.1.2 Other types of assemblies. Fire door assemblies with other types of doors, including swinging elevator doors, hori- zontal sliding fire doors, rolling steel fire doors, fire shutters, bottom- and side-hinged chute intake doors, and top-hinged chute discharge doors, shall be tested in accordance with NFPA 252 or UL 10B. For tests conducted in accordance with NFPA 252, the neutral pressure plane in the furnace shall be maintained as nearly equal to the atmospheric pressure as possible at the top of the door, as specified in the standard. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_1_2_other_door_tests |
| 716.2.1.3 | Glazing in transoms lights and sidelights in corridors and smoke barriers | flag | draft | In a corridor or smoke barrier fire door assembly, transom and sidelight glazing is tested per NFPA 257 or UL 9 with the hose stream: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_1_3_transom_sidelight_glazing). | 716.2.1.3 Glazing in transoms lights and sidelights in corridors and smoke barriers. Glazing material in any other part of the door assembly, including transom lights and sidelights, shall be tested in accordance with NFPA 257 or UL 9, including the hose stream test, in accordance with Section 716.3.1.1. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_1_3_transom_sidelight_glazing |
| 716.2.1.4 | Smoke and draft control | flag | draft | Smoke and draft control fire doors are tested per UL 1784: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_1_4_smoke_draft_control_test). | 716.2.1.4 Smoke and draft control. Fire door assemblies that serve as smoke and draft control assemblies shall be tested in accordance with UL 1784. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_1_4_smoke_draft_control_test |
| 716.2.2 | Performance requirements | flag | draft | Fire doors are installed where Table 716.1(2) says, at its rating: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_2_fire_door_performance). | 716.2.2 Performance requirements. Fire door assemblies shall be installed in the assemblies specified in Table 716.1(2) and shall comply with the fire protection rating specified. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_2_fire_door_performance |
