| 716.2.2.1 | Door assemblies in corridors and smoke barriers | flag | draft | 20-minute doors in rated corridors and smoke barriers are tested without the hose stream, with four exceptions: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_2_1_corridor_smoke_barrier_doors). | 716.2.2.1 Door assemblies in corridors and smoke barriers. Fire door assemblies required to have a minimum fire protection rating of 20 minutes where located in corridor walls or smoke barrier walls having a fire-resistance rating in accordance with Table 716.1(2) shall be tested in accordance with NFPA 252 or UL 10C without the hose stream test. Exceptions: 1. Viewports that require a hole not larger than 1 inch (25 mm) in diameter through the door, have not less than a 0.25-inch-thick (6.4 mm) glass disc and the holder is of metal that will not melt out where subject to temperatures of 1,700°F (927°C). 2. Corridor door assemblies in occupancies of Group I-2 shall be in accordance with Section 407.3.1. 3. Unprotected openings shall be permitted for corridors in multitheater complexes where each motion picture audi- torium has not fewer than one-half of its required exit or exit access doorways opening directly to the exterior or into an exit passageway. 4. Horizontal sliding doors in smoke barriers that comply with Sections 408.6 and 408.8.4 in occupancies in Group I-3. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_2_1_corridor_smoke_barrier_doors |
| 716.2.2.1.1 | Smoke and draft control | flag | draft | Smoke and draft control door assemblies leak at most 3.0 cfm per square foot, no louvers: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_2_1_1_smoke_draft_control). | 716.2.2.1.1 Smoke and draft control. The air leakage rate of the door assembly shall not exceed 3.0 cubic feet per minute per square foot (0.01524 m[fn3]/s × m[fn2]) of door opening at 0.10 inch of water (25 Pa) for both the ambient temperature and elevated temperature tests. Louvers shall be prohibited. Terminated stops shall be prohibited on doors required by Section 405.4.3 to comply with Section 716.2.2.1 and prohibited on doors required by Item 3 of Section 3006.3, or Section 3007.6.3 or 3008.6.3 to comply with this section. Exception: Elevator hoistway door openings protected in accordance with Section 3006.3. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_2_1_1_smoke_draft_control |
| 716.2.2.2 | Door assemblies in other fire partitions | flag | draft | 20-minute doors in other half-hour fire partitions are tested with the hose stream: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_2_2_other_fire_partition_doors). | 716.2.2.2 Door assemblies in other fire partitions. Fire door assemblies required to have a minimum fire protection rating of 20 minutes where located in other fire partitions having a fire-resistance rating of 0.5 hour in accordance with Table 716.1(2) shall be tested in accordance with NFPA 252, UL 10B or UL 10C with the hose stream test. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_2_2_other_fire_partition_doors |
| 716.2.2.3 | Doors in interior exit stairways and ramps and exit passageways | flag | draft | Doors in exit stairways, ramps and passageways transmit at most a 450 degrees F rise at 30 minutes, unless the building is sprinklered throughout: a person's check where the exception isn't stated (general.bc_716_2_2_3_exit_enclosure_door_temperature_rise). | 716.2.2.3 Doors in interior exit stairways and ramps and exit passageways. Fire door assemblies in interior exit stairways and ramps and exit passageways shall have a maximum transmitted temperature rise of not more than 450°F (250°C) above ambient at the end of 30 minutes of standard fire test exposure. Exception: The maximum transmitted temperature rise is not required in buildings equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system installed in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 or 903.3.1.2. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_2_3_exit_enclosure_door_temperature_rise |
| 716.2.2.3.1 | Glazing in doors | flag | draft | In those doors, fire-protection-rated glazing over 100 square inches is not permitted: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_2_3_1_glazing_in_exit_enclosure_doors). | 716.2.2.3.1 Glazing in doors. Fire-protection-rated glazing in excess of 100 square inches (0.065 m[fn2]) is not permitted. Fire- resistance-rated glazing in excess of 100 square inches (0.065 m[fn2]) shall be permitted in fire doors. Listed fire-resistance-rated glazing in a fire door shall have a maximum transmitted temperature rise in accordance with Section 716.2.2.3 when the fire door is tested in accordance with NFPA 252, UL 10B or UL 10C. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_2_3_1_glazing_in_exit_enclosure_doors |
| 716.2.3 | Fire doors | flag | draft | Fire doors are rated as Table 716.1(2) says: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_3_fire_doors). | 716.2.3 Fire doors Fire doors installed within a fire door assembly shall meet the fire rating indicated in Table 716.1(2). | drafted:general.bc_716_2_3_fire_doors |
| 716.2.4 | Fire door frames | flag | draft | Fire door frames are rated as Table 716.1(2) says: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_4_fire_door_frames). | 716.2.4 Fire door frames. Fire door frames installed as part of a fire door assembly shall meet the fire rating indicated in Table 716.1(2). | drafted:general.bc_716_2_4_fire_door_frames |
| 716.2.5.1 | Size limitations | flag | draft | Fire-resistance-rated glazing in a fire door assembly meets the size limits of 716.2.5.1.1; fire-protection-rated glazing meets NFPA 80's size limits and 716.2.5.1.2: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_1_glazing_size_limitations). | 716.2.5.1 Size limitations. Fire-resistance-rated glazing shall comply with the size limitations in Section 716.2.5.1.1. Fire- protection-rated glazing shall comply with the size limitations of NFPA 80, and as provided in Section 716.2.5.1.2. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_1_glazing_size_limitations |
| 716.2.5.1.1 | Fire-resistance-rated glazing in door assemblies in fire walls and fire barriers rated greater than 1 hour | flag | draft | Fire-resistance-rated glazing in fire door assemblies in fire walls and fire barriers (rated more than 1 hour) is tested to ASTM E119 or UL 263 and to NFPA 252, UL 10B or UL 10C, used per Table 716.1(2) up to the maximum size tested and per its listing: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_1_1_fire_resistance_rated_door_glazing). | 716.2.5.1.1 Fire-resistance-rated glazing in door assemblies in fire walls and fire barriers rated greater than 1 hour. Fire-resistance-rated glazing tested to ASTM E119 or UL 263 and NFPA 252, UL 10B or UL 10C shall be permitted in fire door assemblies located in fire walls and in fire barriers in accordance with Table 716.1(2) to the maximum size tested and in accor- dance with their listings. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_1_1_fire_resistance_rated_door_glazing |
| 716.2.5.1.2 | Fire-protection-rated glazing in door assemblies in fire walls and fire barriers rated greater than 1 hour | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing is prohibited in fire walls and fire barriers (rated more than 1 hour) except as 716.2.5.1.2.1 (vision panels of horizontal exits' doors) and 716.2.5.1.2.2 (1 1/2-hour doors in fire barriers) permit: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_1_2_fire_protection_rated_door_glazing). | 716.2.5.1.2 Fire-protection-rated glazing in door assemblies in fire walls and fire barriers rated greater than 1 hour. Fire-protection-rated glazing shall be prohibited in fire walls and fire barriers except as provided in Sections 716.2.5.1.2.1 and 716.2.5.1.2.2. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_1_2_fire_protection_rated_door_glazing |
| 716.2.5.1.2.1 | Horizontal exits | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing is permitted as vision panels in self-closing swinging fire door assemblies serving as horizontal exits in fire walls, limited to 100 square inches: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_1_2_1_horizontal_exit_vision_panels). | 716.2.5.1.2.1 Horizontal exits. Fire-protection-rated glazing shall be permitted as vision panels in self-closing swinging fire door assemblies serving as horizontal exits in fire walls where limited to 100 square inches (0.065 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_1_2_1_horizontal_exit_vision_panels |
| 716.2.5.1.2.2 | Fire barriers | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing is permitted in fire doors with a 1 1/2-hour fire protection rating intended for fire barriers, limited to 100 square inches: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_1_2_2_fire_barrier_door_glazing). | 716.2.5.1.2.2 Fire barriers. Fire-protection-rated glazing shall be permitted in fire doors having a 1 1/2-hour fire protec- tion rating intended for installation in fire barriers, where limited to 100 square inches (0.065 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_1_2_2_fire_barrier_door_glazing |
| 716.2.5.2 | Elevator, stairway and ramp protectives | flag | draft | Approved fire-protection-rated glazing in fire door assemblies in elevator, stairway and ramp enclosures is placed to give a clear view of the passageway or approach to the elevator, stairway or ramp: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_2_stairway_door_vision). | 716.2.5.2 Elevator, stairway and ramp protectives. Approved fire-protection-rated glazing used in fire door assemblies in elevator, stairway and ramp enclosures shall be so located as to furnish clear vision of the passageway or approach to the elevator, stairway or ramp. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_2_stairway_door_vision |
| 716.2.5.3 | Glazing in door assemblies in corridors and smoke barriers | flag | draft | In a 20-minute fire door assembly (in a corridor or smoke barrier), the glazing in the door itself is fire-protection-rated at least 20 minutes and is exempt from the hose stream test: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_3_corridor_door_glazing). | 716.2.5.3 Glazing in door assemblies in corridors and smoke barriers. In a 20-minute fire door assembly, the glazing mate- rial in the door itself shall have a minimum fire-protection-rated glazing of 20 minutes and shall be exempt from the hose stream test. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_3_corridor_door_glazing |
| 716.2.5.4 | Fire door frames with transom lights and sidelights | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing is permitted in door frames with transom lights, sidelights or both where a fire protection rating of 3/4 hour or less is required, and in 2-hour fire-resistance-rated exterior walls, per Table 716.1(2); where a fire protection rating of more than 3/4 hour is required per Table 716.1(2), such frames are permitted with fire-resistance-rated glazing tested as an assembly per ASTM E119 or UL 263: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_5_4_transoms_and_sidelights). | 716.2.5.4 Fire door frames with transom lights and sidelights. Fire-protection-rated glazing shall be permitted in door frames with transom lights, sidelights or both, where a 3/4-hour fire protection rating or less is required and in 2-hour fire-resis- tance-rated exterior walls in accordance with Table 716.1(2). Fire door frames with transom lights, sidelights or both, installed with fire-resistance-rated glazing tested as an assembly in accordance with ASTM E119 or UL 263 shall be permitted where a fire protection rating exceeding 3/4 hour is required in accordance with Table 716.1(2). | drafted:general.bc_716_2_5_4_transoms_and_sidelights |
| 716.2.6.1 | Door closing | flag | draft | Fire doors are latching and self-closing or automatic-closing per 716.2.6; but fire doors in common walls separating dwelling units or sleeping units in Group R-1 may have no automatic- or self-closing devices, elevator car doors and their hoistway doors at the floor designated for recall per 3003.2 may stay open during Phase I emergency recall, and fire doors required only for ICC 500 need not be self-closing or automatic-closing: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_6_1_door_closing). | 716.2.6.1 Door closing. Fire doors shall be latching and self- or automatic-closing in accordance with this section. Exceptions: 1. Fire doors located in common walls separating dwelling units or sleeping units in Group R-1 shall be permitted without automatic- or self-closing devices. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_6_1_door_closing |
| 716.2.6.2 | Latch required | flag | draft | Unless otherwise specifically permitted, a single side-hinged swinging fire door, and both leaves of a pair of them, has an active latch bolt that secures the door when closed: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_6_2_latch_required). | 716.2.6.2 Latch required. Unless otherwise specifically permitted, single side-hinged swinging fire doors and both leaves of pairs of side-hinged swinging fire doors shall be provided with an active latch bolt that will secure the door when it is closed. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_6_2_latch_required |
| 716.2.6.4 | Automatic-closing fire door assemblies | flag | draft | Automatic-closing fire door assemblies are self-closing per NFPA 80: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_6_4_automatic_closing_assemblies). | 716.2.6.4 Automatic-closing fire door assemblies. Automatic-closing fire door assemblies shall be self-closing in accordance with NFPA 80. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_6_4_automatic_closing_assemblies |
| 716.2.6.6 | Smoke-activated doors | flag | draft | Automatic-closing doors may have hold-open devices where they are in walls separating incidental uses (509.4), fire walls (706.8), fire barriers (707.6), fire partitions (708.6), smoke barriers (709.5), smoke partitions (710.5.2.3), shaft enclosures (713.7), or waste and linen chutes, discharge openings and access and discharge rooms (713.13); such doors close automatically on actuation of smoke detectors installed per 907.3, or on loss of power to the smoke detector or hold-open device, starting to close no more than 10 seconds after the smoke detector actuates; loading doors in waste and linen chutes meet 716.2.6.1 and 716.2.6.3: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_6_6_smoke_activated_doors). | 716.2.6.6 Smoke-activated doors. Automatic-closing doors installed in the following locations shall be permitted to have hold-open devices. Doors shall automatically close by the actuation of smoke detectors installed in accordance with Section 907.3 or by loss of power to the smoke detector or hold-open device. Doors that are automatic-closing by smoke detection shall not have more than a 10-second delay before the door starts to close after the smoke detector is actuated. Automatic-closing doors that protect openings installed in the following locations shall comply with this section: 1. In walls that separate incidental uses in accordance with Section 509.4. 2. In fire walls in accordance with Section 706.8. 3. In fire barriers in accordance with Section 707.6. 4. In fire partitions in accordance with Section 708.6. 5. In smoke barriers in accordance with Section 709.5. 6. In smoke partitions in accordance with Section 710.5.2.3. 7. In shaft enclosures in accordance with Section 713.7. 8. In waste and linen chutes, discharge openings and access and discharge rooms in accordance with Section 713.13. Loading doors installed in waste and linen chutes shall meet the requirements of Sections 716.2.6.1 and 716.2.6.3. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_6_6_smoke_activated_doors |
| 716.2.6.7 | Doors in pedestrian ways | flag | draft | Vertical sliding or vertical rolling steel fire doors in openings people walk through are heat activated or activated by smoke detectors with alarm verification: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_6_7_pedestrian_way_doors). | 716.2.6.7 Doors in pedestrian ways. Vertical sliding or vertical rolling steel fire doors in openings through which pedestrians travel shall be heat activated or activated by smoke detectors with alarm verification. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_6_7_pedestrian_way_doors |
| 716.2.7 | Swinging fire shutters | flag | draft | Where swinging fire shutters are installed in exterior openings (an exit stairway's exterior wall among them, 1023.7 and 705.9.2), at least one row in every three vertical rows is arranged to be readily opened from the outside and is identified by distinguishing marks or letters at least 6 inches high: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_7_swinging_fire_shutters). | 716.2.7 Swinging fire shutters. Where fire shutters of the swinging type are installed in exterior openings, not less than one row in every three vertical rows shall be arranged to be readily opened from the outside, and shall be identified by distinguishing marks or letters not less than 6 inches (152 mm) high. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_7_swinging_fire_shutters |
| 716.2.8 | Rolling fire shutters | flag | draft | Where rolling fire shutters are installed (as opening protectives in a rated corridor wall or an exterior wall, Table 716.1(2) and 705.9.2), they include approved automatic-closing devices: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_8_rolling_fire_shutters). | 716.2.8 Rolling fire shutters. Where fire shutters of the rolling type are installed, such shutters shall include approved auto- matic-closing devices. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_8_rolling_fire_shutters |
| 716.2.9 | Labeled protective assemblies | flag | draft | Fire door assemblies are labeled by an approved agency, the labels per NFPA 80, permanently affixed to the door or frame: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_9_labeled_assemblies). | 716.2.9 Labeled protective assemblies. Fire door assemblies shall be labeled by an approved agency. The labels shall comply with NFPA 80, and shall be permanently affixed to the door or frame. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_9_labeled_assemblies |
| 716.2.9.1 | Fire door labeling requirements | flag | draft | A fire door's label shows the manufacturer's name or other identification readily traceable to the manufacturer, the name or trademark of the third-party inspection agency, the fire protection rating and, where 716.2.2.3 requires it for fire doors in interior exit stairways and ramps and exit passageways, the maximum transmitted temperature end point; smoke and draft control doors complying with UL 1784 are labeled as such and meet 716.2.9.3; labels are approved, permanently affixed and applied at the factory or where the door is fabricated and assembled: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_9_1_fire_door_labels). | 716.2.9.1 Fire door labeling requirements. Fire doors shall be labeled showing the name of the manufacturer or other identi- fication readily traceable back to the manufacturer, the name or trademark of the third-party inspection agency, the fire protection rating and, where required for fire doors in interior exit stairways and ramps and exit passageways by Section 716.2.2.3, the maximum transmitted temperature end point. Smoke and draft control doors complying with UL 1784 shall be labeled as such and shall comply with Section 716.2.9.3. Labels shall be approved and permanently affixed. The label shall be applied at the factory or location where fabrication and assembly are performed. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_9_1_fire_door_labels |
| 716.2.9.2 | Oversized doors | flag | draft | An oversized fire door bears an oversized fire door label by an approved agency, or has a certificate of inspection from an approved testing agency stating that it conforms to the requirements of design, materials and construction but has not been subjected to the fire test: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_9_2_oversized_doors). | 716.2.9.2 Oversized doors. Oversized fire doors shall bear an oversized fire door label by an approved agency or shall be provided with a certificate of inspection furnished by an approved testing agency. Where a certificate of inspection is furnished by an approved testing agency, the certificate shall state that the door conforms to the requirements of design, materials and construction, but has not been subjected to the fire test. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_9_2_oversized_doors |
| 716.2.9.3 | Smoke and draft control door labeling requirements | flag | draft | Smoke and draft control doors complying with UL 1784 are labeled per 716.2.9.1 and show the letter "S" on the door's fire-rating label, meaning the door and frame assembly complies where listed or labeled gasketing is installed: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_9_3_smoke_door_labels). | 716.2.9.3 Smoke and draft control door labeling requirements. Smoke and draft control doors complying with UL 1784 shall be labeled in accordance with Section 716.2.9.1 and shall show the letter “S” on the fire-rating label of the door. This marking shall indicate that the door and frame assembly are in compliance where listed or labeled gasketing is installed. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_9_3_smoke_door_labels |
| 716.2.9.4 | Fire door frame labeling requirements | flag | draft | Fire door frames are labeled with the names of the manufacturer and the third-party inspection agency: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_9_4_frame_labels). | 716.2.9.4 Fire door frame labeling requirements. Fire door frames shall be labeled showing the names of the manufacturer and the third-party inspection agency. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_9_4_frame_labels |
| 716.2.9.5 | Labeling | flag | draft | Fire-rated glazing bears a label or other identification, issued by an approved agency and permanently identified on the glazing, showing the manufacturer's name, the test standard and the information Table 716.1(1) requires: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_9_5_glazing_labels). | 716.2.9.5 Labeling. Fire-rated glazing shall bear a label or other identification showing the name of the manufacturer, the test standard and information required in Table 716.1(1) that shall be issued by an approved agency and shall be permanently iden- tified on the glazing. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_9_5_glazing_labels |
| 716.2.9.6 | Fire door operator labeling requirements | flag | draft | Fire door operators for horizontal sliding doors are labeled and listed for use with the assembly: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_9_6_operator_labels). | 716.2.9.6 Fire door operator labeling requirements. Fire door operators for horizontal sliding doors shall be labeled and listed for use with the assembly. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_9_6_operator_labels |
| 716.2.10 | Installation of door assemblies in corridors and smoke barriers | flag | draft | Smoke doors (door assemblies in corridors and smoke barriers) are installed per NFPA 105: a person's check (general.bc_716_2_10_smoke_door_installation). | 716.2.10 Installation of door assemblies in corridors and smoke barriers. Installation of smoke doors shall be in accordance with NFPA 105. | drafted:general.bc_716_2_10_smoke_door_installation |
| 716.3.1 | Testing requirements | flag | draft | Fire window assemblies are of any material or assembly of component materials that meets the tests of 716.3.1.1 and 716.3.1.2 and the fire protection rating Table 716.1(3) gives: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_1_fire_window_testing). | 716.3.1 Testing requirements. Fire window assemblies shall be constructed of any material or assembly of component materials that conforms to the test requirements of Sections 716.3.1.1 and 716.3.1.2 and the fire protection rating indicated in Table 716.1(3). | drafted:general.bc_716_3_1_fire_window_testing |
| 716.3.1.1 | Testing under positive pressure | flag | draft | NFPA 257 or UL 9 evaluates fire-protection-rated glazing under positive pressure: within the first 10 minutes of the test the furnace pressure is set so at least two-thirds of the specimen is above the neutral pressure plane, held at that height for the rest of the test: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_1_1_positive_pressure_test). | 716.3.1.1 Testing under positive pressure. NFPA 257 or UL 9 shall evaluate fire-protection-rated glazing under positive pres- sure. Within the first 10 minutes of a test, the pressure in the furnace shall be adjusted so not less than two-thirds of the test specimen is above the neutral pressure plane, and the neutral pressure plane shall be maintained at that height for the balance of the test. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_1_1_positive_pressure_test |
| 716.3.1.2 | Nonsymmetrical glazing systems | flag | draft | Nonsymmetrical fire-protection-rated glazing systems in fire partitions, fire barriers or exterior walls with a fire separation distance of 10 feet or less per Section 705 are tested with both faces exposed to the furnace, the rating the shorter of the two tests per NFPA 257 or UL 9: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_1_2_nonsymmetrical_glazing). | 716.3.1.2 Nonsymmetrical glazing systems. Nonsymmetrical fire-protection-rated glazing systems in fire partitions, fire barriers or in exterior walls with a fire separation distance of 10 feet (3048 mm) or less pursuant to Section 705 shall be tested with both faces exposed to the furnace, and the assigned fire protection rating shall be the shortest duration obtained from the two tests conducted in compliance with NFPA 257 or UL 9. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_1_2_nonsymmetrical_glazing |
| 716.3.2 | Performance requirements | flag | draft | Fire window assemblies are installed in the assemblies and have the fire protection rating Table 716.1(3) gives: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_2_fire_window_performance). | 716.3.2 Performance requirements. Fire window assemblies shall be installed in the assemblies and comply with the fire protec- tion rating specified in Table 716.1(3). | drafted:general.bc_716_3_2_fire_window_performance |
| 716.3.2.1 | Interior fire window assemblies | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing in fire window assemblies in fire partitions and fire barriers is used only in assemblies with a fire-resistance rating of at most 1 hour, per 716.3.2.1.1 to 716.3.2.1.3: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_2_1_interior_fire_windows). | 716.3.2.1 Interior fire window assemblies. Fire-protection-rated glazing used in fire window assemblies located in fire parti- tions and fire barriers shall be limited to use in assemblies with a maximum fire-resistance rating of 1 hour in accordance with this section. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_2_1_interior_fire_windows |
| 716.3.2.1.1 | Where 3/4-hour-fire-protection window assemblies permitted | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing needing 45-minute opening protection per Table 716.1(3) is limited to fire partitions per Section 708 and to fire barriers used as 707.3.6, 707.3.7 and 707.3.9 set out, where the fire-resistance rating is at most 1 hour; fire-resistance-rated glazing assemblies tested per ASTM E119 or UL 263 are not so limited: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_2_1_1_three_quarter_hour_windows). | 716.3.2.1.1 Where 3/4-hour-fire-protection window assemblies permitted. Fire-protection-rated glazing requiring 45- minute opening protection in accordance with Table 716.1(3) shall be limited to fire partitions designed in accordance with Section 708 and fire barriers utilized in the applications set forth in Sections 707.3.6, 707.3.7 and 707.3.9 where the fire-resis- tance rating does not exceed 1 hour. Fire-resistance-rated glazing assemblies tested in accordance with ASTM E119 or UL 263 shall not be subject to the limitations of this section. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_2_1_1_three_quarter_hour_windows |
| 716.3.2.1.2 | Area limitations | flag | draft | The total glazing area of fire-protection-rated window assemblies is at most 25 percent of the area of a common wall with any room: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_2_1_2_fire_window_area). | 716.3.2.1.2 Area limitations. The total area of the glazing in fire-protection-rated window assemblies shall not exceed 25 percent of the area of a common wall with any room. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_2_1_2_fire_window_area |
| 716.3.3 | Fire window frames | flag | draft | Fire window frames installed with a fire window assembly have the fire protection rating Table 716.1(3) gives: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_3_fire_window_frames). | 716.3.3 Fire window frames. Fire window frames installed with a fire window assembly shall meet the fire-protection rating indi- cated in Table 716.1(3). | drafted:general.bc_716_3_3_fire_window_frames |
| 716.3.3.1 | Window mullions | flag | draft | Metal mullions of a nominal height of more than 12 feet are protected with materials giving the same fire-resistance rating as the wall the protective is in: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_3_1_window_mullions). | 716.3.3.1 Window mullions. Metal mullions that exceed a nominal height of 12 feet (3658 mm) shall be protected with materi- als to afford the same fire-resistance rating as required for the wall construction in which the protective is located. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_3_1_window_mullions |
| 716.3.4 | Fire-protection-rated glazing | flag | draft | Glazing in fire window assemblies is fire protection rated per this section and Table 716.1(3), tested to and meeting the acceptance criteria of NFPA 257 or UL 9; openings in exterior wall assemblies that are not fire-resistance rated, where 705.3, 705.9, 705.9.5 or 705.9.6 requires them protected, have a fire protection rating of at least 3/4 hour; fire-protection-rated glazing in 1/2-hour fire-resistance-rated partitions may have a 20-minute rating: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_4_fire_window_glazing). | 716.3.4 Fire-protection-rated glazing. Glazing in fire window assemblies shall be fire protection rated in accordance with this section and Table 716.1(3). Fire-protection-rated glazing in fire window assemblies shall be tested in accordance with and shall meet the acceptance criteria of NFPA 257 or UL 9. Openings in nonfire-resistance-rated exterior wall assemblies that require protection in accordance with Section 705.3, 705.9, 705.9.5 or 705.9.6 shall have a fire protection rating of not less than 3/4 hour. Fire-protection-rated glazing in 1/2-hour fire-resistance-rated partitions is permitted to have a 20-minute fire protection rating. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_4_fire_window_glazing |
| 716.3.4.1 | Glass and glazing | flag | draft | Glazing in fire window assemblies is fire-protection-rated glazing installed per, and within the size limits of, NFPA 80: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_4_1_glass_and_glazing). | 716.3.4.1 Glass and glazing. Glazing in fire window assemblies shall be fire-protection-rated glazing installed in accordance with and complying with the size limitations set forth in NFPA 80. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_4_1_glass_and_glazing |
| 716.3.5 | Labeled protective assemblies | flag | draft | Glazing in fire window assemblies is labeled by an approved agency, the labels per NFPA 80 and 716.3.5.2: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_5_labeled_window_assemblies). | 716.3.5 Labeled protective assemblies. Glazing in fire window assemblies shall be labeled by an approved agency. The labels shall comply with NFPA 80 and Section 716.3.5.2. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_5_labeled_window_assemblies |
| 716.3.5.1 | Fire window frames | flag | draft | Fire window frames are approved for the intended application: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_5_1_fire_window_frames_approved). | 716.3.5.1 Fire window frames. Fire window frames shall be approved for the intended application. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_5_1_fire_window_frames_approved |
| 716.3.5.2 | Labeling requirements | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing bears a label or other identification, issued by an approved agency and permanently identified on the glazing, showing the manufacturer's name, the test standard and the information 716.1.2.2.2 and Table 716.1(3) require: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_5_2_fire_window_labels). | 716.3.5.2 Labeling requirements. Fire-protection-rated glazing shall bear a label or other identification showing the name of the manufacturer, the test standard and information required in Section 716.1.2.2.2 and Table 716.1(3) that shall be issued by an approved agency and permanently identified on the glazing. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_5_2_fire_window_labels |
| 716.3.6.1 | Closure | flag | draft | Fire-protection-rated glazing is in the fixed position or automatic-closing, installed in labeled frames: a person's check (general.bc_716_3_6_1_fire_window_closure). | 716.3.6.1 Closure. Fire-protection-rated glazing shall be in the fixed position or be automatic-closing and shall be installed in labeled frames. | drafted:general.bc_716_3_6_1_fire_window_closure |
| 716.4 | Fire protective curtain assembly | flag | draft | Approved fire protective curtain assemblies are of any materials or assembly of component materials tested without hose stream per UL 10D, and meet 716.4.1 to 716.4.3: a person's check (general.bc_716_4_fire_protective_curtains). | 716.4 Fire protective curtain assembly. Approved fire protective curtain assemblies shall be constructed of any materials or assem- bly of component materials tested without hose stream in accordance with UL 10D, and shall comply with the Sections 716.4.1 through 716.4.3. | drafted:general.bc_716_4_fire_protective_curtains |
| 716.4.1 | Label | flag | draft | Fire protective curtain assemblies used as opening protectives in fire-rated walls and smoke partitions are labeled per 716.2.9: a person's check (general.bc_716_4_1_curtain_labels). | 716.4.1 Label. Fire protective curtain assemblies used as opening protectives in fire-rated walls and smoke partitions shall be labeled in accordance with Section 716.2.9. | drafted:general.bc_716_4_1_curtain_labels |
| 716.4.2 | Smoke and draft control | flag | draft | Fire protective curtain assemblies protecting openings where smoke and draft control assemblies are required meet 716.2.1.4: a person's check (general.bc_716_4_2_curtain_smoke_control). | 716.4.2 Smoke and draft control. Fire protective curtain assemblies used to protect openings where smoke and draft control assemblies are required shall comply with Section 716.2.1.4. | drafted:general.bc_716_4_2_curtain_smoke_control |
| 716.4.3 | Installation | flag | draft | Fire protective curtain assemblies are installed per NFPA 80: a person's check (general.bc_716_4_3_curtain_installation). | 716.4.3 Installation. Fire protective curtain assemblies shall be installed in accordance with NFPA 80. | drafted:general.bc_716_4_3_curtain_installation |
| 903.1.1 | Alternative protection | flag | draft | An alternative fire-extinguishing system per 904 instead of sprinklers, where the standard recognizes it and the fire code official approves: a person's check (general.bc_903_1_1_alternative_protection). | [F] 903.1.1 Alternative protection. Alternative automatic fire-extinguishing systems complying with Section 904 shall be permit- ted instead of automatic sprinkler system protection where recognized by the applicable standard and approved by the fire code official. | drafted:general.bc_903_1_1_alternative_protection |
| 903.2 | Where required | flag | draft | Says sprinkler systems are required in new buildings where 903.2.1 to 903.2.12 say, each group's rule reading it; in a building of two or more groups each portion's fire areas, and the exception for spaces used only for telecommunications equipment, are a person's check (general.bc_903_2_mixed_occupancies). | [F] 903.2 Where required. Approved automatic sprinkler systems in new buildings and structures shall be provided in the locations described in Sections 903.2.1 through 903.2.12. Exception: Spaces or areas in telecommunications buildings used exclusively for telecommunications equipment, associated electrical power distribution equipment, batteries not required to have an automatic sprinkler system by Section 1207 of the Fire Code of New York State for energy storage systems and standby engines, provided that those spaces or areas are equipped throughout with an automatic smoke detection system in accordance with Section 907.2 and are separated from the remainder of the building by not less than 1-hour fire barriers constructed in accordance with Section 707 or not less than 2-hour horizontal assemblies constructed in accordance with Section 711, or both. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_mixed_occupancies |
| 903.2.1.1 | Group A-1 | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group A-1 fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet or has an occupant load of 300 or more, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_1_1_group_a1_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.1.1 Group A-1. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout stories containing Group A-1 occupancies and throughout all stories from the Group A-1 occupancy to and including the levels of exit discharge serving that occupancy where one of the following conditions exists: 1. The fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]). 2. The fire area has an occupant load of 300 or more. 3. The fire area is located on a floor other than a level of exit discharge serving such occupancies. 4. The fire area contains a multitheater complex. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_1_group_a1_sprinklers |
| 903.2.1.2 | Group A-2 | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group A-2 fire area exceeds 5,000 square feet or has an occupant load of 100 or more, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_1_2_group_a2_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.1.2 Group A-2. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout stories containing Group A-2 occupancies and throughout all stories from the Group A-2 occupancy to and including the levels of exit discharge serving that occupancy where one of the following conditions exists: 1. The fire area exceeds 5,000 square feet (464 m[fn2]). 2. The fire area has an occupant load of 100 or more. 3. The fire area is located on a floor other than a level of exit discharge serving such occupancies. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_2_group_a2_sprinklers |
| 903.2.1.3 | Group A-3 | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group A-3 fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet or has an occupant load of 300 or more, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_1_3_group_a3_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.1.3 Group A-3. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout stories containing Group A-3 occupancies and throughout all stories from the Group A-3 occupancy to and including the levels of exit discharge serving that occupancy where one of the following conditions exists: 1. The fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]). 2. The fire area has an occupant load of 300 or more. 3. The fire area is located on a floor other than a level of exit discharge serving such occupancies. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_3_group_a3_sprinklers |
| 903.2.1.4 | Group A-4 | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group A-4 fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet or has an occupant load of 300 or more, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_1_4_group_a4_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.1.4 Group A-4. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout stories containing Group A-4 occupancies and throughout all stories from the Group A-4 occupancy to and including the levels of exit discharge serving that occupancy where one of the following conditions exists: 1. The fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]). 2. The fire area has an occupant load of 300 or more. 3. The fire area is located on a floor other than a level of exit discharge serving such occupancies. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_4_group_a4_sprinklers |
| 903.2.1.5 | Group A-5 | flag | draft | Enclosed Group A-5 accessory use areas over 1,000 square feet are sprinklered: which enclosed areas are accessory use areas is a person's check (general.bc_903_2_1_5_a5_accessory_areas). | [F] 903.2.1.5 Group A-5. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided for all enclosed Group A-5 accessory use areas in excess of 1,000 square feet (93 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_5_a5_accessory_areas |
| 903.2.1.5.1 | Spaces under grandstands or bleachers | flag | draft | Enclosed spaces under grandstands or bleachers are sprinklered per 903.3.1.1 where they exceed 1,000 square feet, or are 1,000 square feet or less and not built per 1030.1.1.1: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_1_5_1_under_grandstands). | [F] 903.2.1.5.1 Spaces under grandstands or bleachers. Enclosed spaces under grandstands or bleachers shall be equipped with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 where either of the following exist: | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_5_1_under_grandstands |
| 903.2.1.6 | Assembly occupancies on roofs | flag | draft | Where an occupiable roof has an assembly occupancy of more than 100 (Group A-2) or 300 (other Group A), every floor between the roof and the level of exit discharge has a system per 903.3.1.1 or 903.3.1.2, open parking garages of Type I or II construction aside: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_1_6_assembly_on_roofs). | [F] 903.2.1.6 Assembly occupancies on roofs. Where an occupiable roof has an assembly occupancy with an occupant load exceeding 100 for Group A-2 and 300 for other Group A occupancies, all floors between the occupiable roof and the level of exit discharge shall be equipped with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 or 903.3.1.2. Exception: Open parking garages of Type I or Type II construction. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_6_assembly_on_roofs |
| 903.2.1.7 | Multiple fire areas | flag | draft | Where Group A-1 to A-4 fire areas share exit or exit access components and together hold 300 or more, a sprinkler system: the fire areas aren't in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_1_7_multiple_a_fire_areas). | [F] 903.2.1.7 Multiple fire areas. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided where multiple fire areas of Group A-1, A-2, A-3 or A-4 occupancies share exit or exit access components and the combined occupant load of theses fire areas is 300 or more. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_7_multiple_a_fire_areas |
| 903.2.2.1 | Ambulatory care facilities | flag | draft | The whole floor holding an ambulatory care facility is sprinklered where four or more care recipients are incapable of self-preservation, or one or more are at other than the level of exit discharge; where care is on another level, that floor, every floor between it and the nearest level of exit discharge, that level and every floor below it too (open parking garage floors aside): not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_2_1_ambulatory_care). | [F] 903.2.2.1 Ambulatory care facilities. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed throughout the entire floor contain- ing an ambulatory care facility where either of the following conditions exist at any time: 1. Four or more care recipients are incapable of self-preservation. 2. One or more care recipients that are incapable of self-preservation are located at other than the level of exit discharge serving such a facility. In buildings where ambulatory care is provided on levels other than the level of exit discharge, an automatic sprinkler system shall be installed throughout the entire floor as well as all floors below where such care is provided, and all floors between the level of ambulatory care and the nearest level of exit discharge, the level of exit discharge, and all floors below the level of exit discharge. Exception: Floors classified as an open parking garage are not required to be sprinklered. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_2_1_ambulatory_care |
| 903.2.2.2 | Laboratories involving testing, research and development | flag | draft | Fire areas used for research and development or testing of lithium-ion or lithium metal batteries are sprinklered throughout: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_2_2_battery_laboratories). | [F] 903.2.2.2 Laboratories involving testing, research and development. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed throughout the fire areas utilized for the research and development or testing of lithium-ion or lithium metal batteries. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_2_2_battery_laboratories |
| 903.2.3 | Group E | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group E fire area is greater than 12,000 square feet or has an occupant load of 300 or more, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_3_group_e_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.3 Group E. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided for Group E occupancies as follows: 1. Throughout all Group E fire areas greater than 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]) in area. 2. The Group E fire area is located on a floor other than a level of exit discharge serving such occupancies. Exception: In buildings where every classroom has not fewer than one exterior exit door at ground level, an automatic sprinkler system is not required in any area below the lowest level of exit discharge serving that area. 3. The Group E fire area has an occupant load of 300 or more. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_3_group_e_sprinklers |
| 903.2.4 | Group F-1 | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group F-1 fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_4_group_f1_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.4 Group F-1. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout all buildings containing a Group F-1 occu- pancy where one of the following conditions exists: 1. A Group F-1 fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]). 2. A Group F-1 fire area is located more than three stories above grade plane. 3. The combined area of all Group F-1 fire areas on all floors, including any mezzanines, exceeds 24,000 square feet (2230 m[fn2]). 4. A Group F-1 occupancy is used to manufacture lithium-ion or lithium metal batteries. 5. A Group F-1 occupancy is used to manufacture vehicles, energy storage systems or equipment containing lithium-ion or lithium metal batteries where the batteries are installed as part of the manufacturing process. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_4_group_f1_sprinklers |
| 903.2.4.1 | Woodworking operations | flag | draft | Group F-1 fire areas with woodworking over 2,500 square feet that makes or uses finely divided combustible material are sprinklered throughout: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_4_1_woodworking). | [F] 903.2.4.1 Woodworking operations. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout all Group F-1 occupancy fire areas that contain woodworking operations in excess of 2,500 square feet (232 m[fn2]) in area that generate finely divided combustible waste or use finely divided combustible materials. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_4_1_woodworking |
| 903.2.4.2 | Group F-1 distilled spirits | flag | draft | A Group F-1 fire area making distilled spirits is sprinklered throughout: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_4_2_distilled_spirits). | [F] 903.2.4.2 Group F-1 distilled spirits. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout a Group F-1 fire area used for the manufacture of distilled spirits. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_4_2_distilled_spirits |
| 903.2.4.3 | Group F-1 upholstered furniture or mattresses | flag | draft | A Group F-1 fire area over 2,500 square feet making upholstered furniture or mattresses is sprinklered throughout: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_4_3_upholstered_furniture). | [F] 903.2.4.3 Group F-1 upholstered furniture or mattresses. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout a Group F-1 fire area that exceeds 2,500 square feet (232 m[fn2]) used for the manufacture of upholstered furniture or mattresses. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_4_3_upholstered_furniture |
| 903.2.5 | Group H | reference | draft | Says Group H's sprinkler systems are as 903.2.5.1 to 903.2.5.3 require: a pointer, no requirement of its own. | [F] 903.2.5 Group H. Automatic sprinkler systems shall be provided in high-hazard occupancies as required in Sections 903.2.5.1 through 903.2.5.3. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_1_group_h_sprinklers |
| 903.2.5.1 | General | rule | draft | Group H requires a sprinkler system, whatever its size: a rule on the stated groups and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_5_1_group_h_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.5.1 General. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed in Group H occupancies. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_1_group_h_sprinklers |
| 903.2.5.2 | Group H-5 occupancies | flag | draft | A building containing Group H-5 sprinklered throughout, its design per Table 903.2.5.2: the design isn't in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_5_2_h5_sprinkler_design). | [F] 903.2.5.2 Group H-5 occupancies. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed throughout buildings containing Group H-5 occupancies. The design of the automatic sprinkler system shall be not less than that required by this code for the occu- pancy hazard classifications in accordance with Table 903.2.5.2. Where the design area of the automatic sprinkler system consists of a corridor protected by one row of sprinklers, the maxi- mum number of sprinklers required to be calculated is 13. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_2_h5_sprinkler_design |
| Table 903.2.5.2 | GROUP H-5 AUTOMATIC SPRINKLER SYSTEM DESIGN CRITERIA | reference | draft | The hazard classes 903.2.5.2 sends Group H-5's sprinkler design to, quoted by its flag (general.bc_903_2_5_2_h5_sprinkler_design). | [F] TABLE 903.2.5.2—GROUP H-5 AUTOMATIC SPRINKLER SYSTEM DESIGN CRITERIA LOCATION OCCUPANCY HAZARD CLASSIFICATION Fabrication areas Ordinary Hazard Group 2 Service corridors Ordinary Hazard Group 2 Storage rooms without dispensing Ordinary Hazard Group 2 Storage rooms with dispensing Extra Hazard Group 2 Corridors Ordinary Hazard Group 2 | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_2_h5_sprinkler_design |
| 903.2.5.3 | Pyroxylin plastics | flag | draft | A sprinkler system where over 100 pounds of cellulose nitrate film or pyroxylin plastics are kept, in any building: the contents aren't in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_5_3_pyroxylin_plastics). | [F] 903.2.5.3 Pyroxylin plastics. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided in buildings, or portions thereof, where cellu- lose nitrate film or pyroxylin plastics are manufactured, stored or handled in quantities exceeding 100 pounds (45 kg). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_3_pyroxylin_plastics |
| 903.2.6 | Group I | rule | draft | Group I requires a sprinkler system throughout, its exceptions Group I-1 Condition 1's NFPA 13R and Group I-4's day care at the level of exit discharge: a rule on the stated groups and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_6_group_i_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.6 Group I. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout buildings with a Group I fire area. Exceptions: 1. An automatic sprinkler system installed in accordance with Section 903.3.1.2 shall be permitted in Group I-1, Condi- tion 1 facilities. 2. An automatic sprinkler system is not required where Group I-4 day care facilities are at the level of exit discharge and where every room where care is provided has not fewer than one exterior exit door. 3. In buildings where Group I-4 day care is provided on levels other than the level of exit discharge, an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 shall be installed on the entire floor where care is provided, all floors between the level of care and the level of exit discharge, and all floors below the level of exit discharge other than areas classified as an open parking garage. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_6_group_i_sprinklers |
| 903.2.7 | Group M | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group M fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_7_group_m_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.7 Group M. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout buildings containing a Group M occupancy where one of the following conditions exists: 1. A Group M fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]). 2. A Group M fire area is located more than three stories above grade plane. 3. The combined area of all Group M fire areas on all floors, including any mezzanines, exceeds 24,000 square feet (2230 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_7_group_m_sprinklers |
| 903.2.7.1 | High-piled storage | flag | draft | A Group M building storing merchandise in high-piled or rack storage arrays has a sprinkler system per the Fire Code of New York State: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_7_1_high_piled_storage). | [F] 903.2.7.1 High-piled storage. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided in accordance with the Fire Code of New York State in all buildings of Group M where storage of merchandise is in high-piled or rack storage arrays. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_7_1_high_piled_storage |
| 903.2.7.2 | Group M upholstered furniture or mattresses | flag | draft | A Group M fire area whose display and sale of upholstered furniture or mattresses exceeds 5,000 square feet is sprinklered throughout: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_7_2_upholstered_furniture). | [F] 903.2.7.2 Group M upholstered furniture or mattresses. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout a Group M fire area where the area used for the display and sale of upholstered furniture or mattresses exceeds 5,000 square feet (464 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_7_2_upholstered_furniture |
| 903.2.7.3 | Lithium-ion or lithium metal battery storage | flag | draft | A room or space in Group M storing lithium-ion or lithium metal batteries is sprinklered where Section 320 or Chapter 32 of the Fire Code of New York State requires it: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_7_3_battery_storage). | [F] 903.2.7.3 Lithium-ion or lithium metal battery storage. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided in a room or space within a Group M occupancy where required for the storage of lithium-ion or lithium metal batteries by Section 320 of the Fire Code of New York State or Chapter 32 of the Fire Code of New York State. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_7_3_battery_storage |
| 903.2.8 | Group R | rule | draft | Every building with a Group R fire area requires a sprinkler system per 903.3: a rule on the stated groups, sprinkler system and stories (general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.8 Group R. An automatic sprinkler system installed in accordance with Section 903.3 shall be provided throughout all buildings with a Group R fire area. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers |
| 903.2.8.1 | Group R-3 | reference | draft | Permits NFPA 13D in Group R-3: read by 903.2.8's rule (general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.8.1 Group R-3. An automatic sprinkler system installed in accordance with Section 903.3.1.3 shall be permitted in Group R-3 occupancies. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers |
| 903.2.8.2 | Group R-4, Condition 1 | reference | draft | Permits NFPA 13D in Group R-4 Condition 1: read by 903.2.8's rule, which needs review there (the condition isn't stated). | [F] 903.2.8.2 Group R-4, Condition 1. An automatic sprinkler system installed in accordance with Section 903.3.1.3 shall be permitted in Group R-4, Condition 1 occupancies. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers |
| 903.2.8.3 | Care facilities | reference | draft | Permits NFPA 13D in care facilities for five or fewer in a single-family dwelling, which 308.2.4 and 310.4.1 class as Group R-3: read by 903.2.8's rule. | [F] 903.2.8.3 Care facilities. An automatic sprinkler system installed in accordance with Section 903.3.1.3 shall be permitted in care facilities with five or fewer individuals in a single-family dwelling. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers |
| 903.2.9 | Group S-1 | rule | draft | A sprinkler system where a Group S-1 fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet, among other conditions: a rule per room, on the stated group and sprinkler system (general.bc_903_2_9_group_s1_sprinklers). | [F] 903.2.9 Group S-1. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout all buildings containing a Group S-1 occu- pancy where one of the following conditions exists: 1. A Group S-1 fire area exceeds 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]). 2. A Group S-1 fire area is located more than three stories above grade plane. 3. The combined area of all Group S-1 fire areas on all floors, including any mezzanines, exceeds 24,000 square feet (2230 m[fn2]). 4. A Group S-1 fire area used for the storage of commercial motor vehicles where the fire area exceeds 5,000 square feet (464 m[fn2]). 5. A Group S-1 fire area used for the storage of lithium-ion or lithium metal powered vehicles where the fire area exceeds 500 square feet (46.4 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_9_group_s1_sprinklers |
| 903.2.9.1 | Repair garages | flag | draft | A building used as a repair garage per Section 406 is sprinklered throughout where it has two or more stories above grade plane (basements included) and a repair garage fire area over 10,000 square feet, one story with one over 12,000 square feet, vehicles serviced in a basement, commercial motor vehicles repaired in a fire area over 5,000 square feet, or lithium-ion or lithium metal powered vehicles stored in one over 500 square feet: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_9_1_repair_garages). | [F] 903.2.9.1 Repair garages. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout all buildings used as repair garages in accordance with Section 406, as shown: 1. Buildings having two or more stories above grade plane, including basements, with a fire area containing a repair garage exceeding 10,000 square feet (929 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_9_1_repair_garages |
| 903.2.9.2 | Bulk storage of tires | flag | draft | A building or structure storing more than 20,000 cubic feet of tires is sprinklered throughout per 903.3.1.1: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_9_2_bulk_tires). | [F] 903.2.9.2 Bulk storage of tires. Buildings and structures where the area for the storage of tires exceeds 20,000 cubic feet (566 m[fn3]) shall be equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_9_2_bulk_tires |
| 903.2.9.3 | Group S-1 Distilled spirits or wine | flag | draft | A Group S-1 fire area used for bulk storage of distilled spirits or wine is sprinklered throughout: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_9_3_distilled_spirits_wine). | [F] 903.2.9.3 Group S-1 Distilled spirits or wine. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout a Group S-1 fire area used for the bulk storage of distilled spirits or wine. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_9_3_distilled_spirits_wine |
| 903.2.9.4 | Group S-1 upholstered furniture and mattresses | flag | draft | A Group S-1 fire area storing upholstered furniture or mattresses over 2,500 square feet is sprinklered throughout, self-service storage of one story above grade plane with every space reached from outside aside: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_9_4_upholstered_furniture). | [F] 903.2.9.4 Group S-1 upholstered furniture and mattresses. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout a Group S-1 fire area where the area used for the storage of upholstered furniture or mattresses exceeds 2,500 square feet (232 m[fn2]). Exception: Self-service storage facilities not greater than one story above grade plane where all storage spaces can be accessed directly from the exterior. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_9_4_upholstered_furniture |
| 903.2.10 | Group S-2 parking garages | flag | draft | A building classified as a parking garage is sprinklered throughout where an enclosed parking garage per 406.6 has a fire area over 12,000 square feet or is beneath other groups (beneath Group R-3 aside), or an open parking garage per 406.5 has one over 48,000 square feet: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_10_parking_garages). | [F] 903.2.10 Group S-2 parking garages. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout buildings classified as park- ing garages where any of the following conditions exists: 1. Where the fire area of the enclosed parking garage in accordance with Section 406.6 exceeds 12,000 square feet (1115 m[fn2]). 2. Where the enclosed parking garage in accordance with Section 406.6 is located beneath other groups. Exception: Enclosed parking garages located beneath Group R-3 occupancies. 3. Where the fire area of the open parking garage in accordance with Section 406.5 exceeds 48,000 square feet (4460 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_10_parking_garages |
| 903.2.10.1 | Commercial parking garages | flag | draft | A building storing commercial motor vehicles is sprinklered throughout where the fire area exceeds 5,000 square feet: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_10_1_commercial_parking). | [F] 903.2.10.1 Commercial parking garages. An automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout buildings used for storage of commercial motor vehicles where the fire area exceeds 5,000 square feet (464 m[fn2]). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_10_1_commercial_parking |
| 903.2.10.2 | Mechanical-access enclosed parking garages | flag | draft | A building storing motor vehicles in a mechanical-access enclosed parking garage has an approved sprinkler system throughout, the garage's portion a specially engineered one: not in the model, a person's check (general.bc_903_2_10_2_mechanical_access_garages). | [F] 903.2.10.2 Mechanical-access enclosed parking garages. An approved automatic sprinkler system shall be provided throughout buildings used for the storage of motor vehicles in a mechanical-access enclosed parking garage. The portion of the building that contains the mechanical-access enclosed parking garage shall be protected with a specially engineered automatic sprinkler system. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_10_2_mechanical_access_garages |
| 903.2.11.1 | Stories without openings | flag | draft | A story (basements included) over 1,500 square feet is sprinklered throughout unless, in each 50 feet of exterior wall on at least one side, it has openings below grade reached by an exterior stairway per 1011 or a ramp ...: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_1_stories_without_openings). | [F] 903.2.11.1 Stories without openings. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed throughout all stories, including basements, of all buildings where the floor area exceeds 1,500 square feet (139.4 m[fn2]) and where the story does not comply with the following criteria for exterior wall openings: 1. Openings below grade that lead directly to ground level by an exterior stairway complying with Section 1011 or an outside ramp complying with Section 1012. Openings shall be located in each 50 linear feet (15 240 mm), or fraction thereof, of exterior wall in the story on not fewer than one side. The required openings shall be distributed such that the lineal distance between adjacent openings does not exceed 50 feet (15 240 mm). 2. Openings entirely above the adjoining ground level totaling not less than 20 square feet (1.86 m[fn2]) in each 50 linear feet (15 240 mm), or fraction thereof, of exterior wall in the story on not fewer than one side. The required openings shall be distributed such that the lineal distance between adjacent openings does not exceed 50 feet (15 240 mm). The height of the bottom of the clear opening shall not exceed 44 inches (1118 mm) measured from the floor. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_1_stories_without_openings |
| 903.2.11.1.1 | Opening dimensions and access | flag | draft | The openings 903.2.11.1 counts are at least 30 inches in each dimension, the fire department has access to them from outside, unobstructed for firefighting or rescue: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_1_1_opening_dimensions). | [F] 903.2.11.1.1 Opening dimensions and access. Openings shall have a minimum dimension of not less than 30 inches (762 mm). Access to such openings shall be provided for the fire department from the exterior and shall not be obstructed in a manner such that firefighting or rescue cannot be accomplished from the exterior. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_1_1_opening_dimensions |
| 903.2.11.1.2 | Openings on one side only | flag | draft | Where a story's openings are on one side only and the opposite wall is more than 75 feet from them, the story is sprinklered throughout, or has openings on at least two sides: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_1_2_openings_on_one_side). | [F] 903.2.11.1.2 Openings on one side only. Where openings in a story are provided on only one side and the opposite wall of such story is more than 75 feet (22 860 mm) from such openings, the story shall be equipped throughout with an approved automatic sprinkler system, or openings shall be provided on not fewer than two sides of the story. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_1_2_openings_on_one_side |
| 903.2.11.1.3 | Basements | flag | draft | Where any part of a basement is more than 75 feet from the openings 903.2.11.1 requires, or walls, partitions or other obstructions restrict hose streams, the basement is sprinklered throughout: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_1_3_basements). | [F] 903.2.11.1.3 Basements. Where any portion of a basement is located more than 75 feet (22 860 mm) from openings required by Section 903.2.11.1, or where walls, partitions or other obstructions are installed that restrict the application of water from hose streams, the basement shall be equipped throughout with an approved automatic sprinkler system. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_1_3_basements |
| 903.2.11.2 | Rubbish and linen chutes | flag | draft | Rubbish and linen chutes have sprinklers at the top, in their terminal rooms, at alternate floors and the lowest intake, and a chute's extension more than one floor below the lowest intake recessed, freeze-protected spri...: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_2_chutes). | [F] 903.2.11.2 Rubbish and linen chutes. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed at the top of rubbish and linen chutes and in their terminal rooms. Chutes shall have additional sprinkler heads installed at alternate floors and at the lowest intake. Where a rubbish chute extends through a building more than one floor below the lowest intake, the extension shall have sprinklers installed that are recessed from the drop area of the chute and protected from freezing in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1. Such sprinklers shall be installed at alternate floors, beginning with the second level below the last intake and ending with the floor above the discharge. Access to sprinklers in chutes shall be provided for servicing. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_2_chutes |
| 903.2.11.3 | Buildings 55 feet or more in height | flag | draft | A building with a story whose occupant load is 30 or more and whose finished floor is 55 feet or more above the lowest level of fire department vehicle access is sprinklered throughout (Group F-2 aside): the fire departm...: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_3_buildings_55_feet). | [F] 903.2.11.3 Buildings 55 feet or more in height. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed throughout buildings that have one or more stories with an occupant load of 30 or more located 55 feet (16 764 mm) or more above the lowest level of fire department vehicle access, measured to the finished floor. Exception: Occupancies in Group F-2. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_3_buildings_55_feet |
| 903.2.11.4 | Ducts conveying hazardous exhausts | flag | draft | Where the Mechanical Code of New York State requires it, ducts conveying hazardous exhaust or flammable or combustible materials are sprinklered, ducts under 10 inches across aside: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_4_hazardous_exhaust_ducts). | [F] 903.2.11.4 Ducts conveying hazardous exhausts. Where required by the Mechanical Code of New York State, automatic sprinklers shall be provided in ducts conveying hazardous exhaust or flammable or combustible materials. Exception: Ducts where the largest cross-sectional diameter of the duct is less than 10 inches (254 mm). | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_4_hazardous_exhaust_ducts |
| 903.2.11.5 | Commercial cooking operations | flag | draft | Where a sprinkler system is used to comply with Section 904, commercial kitchen exhaust hood and duct systems are sprinklered: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_5_commercial_cooking). | [F] 903.2.11.5 Commercial cooking operations. An automatic sprinkler system shall be installed in commercial kitchen exhaust hood and duct systems where an automatic sprinkler system is used to comply with Section 904. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_5_commercial_cooking |
| 903.2.11.6 | Other required fire protection systems | flag | draft | Table 903.2.11.6's sections require sprinklers for their buildings and areas: covered and open malls (402.5, 402.6.2), high-rise buildings (403.3), atriums (404.3), underground structures (405.3), Group I-2 (407.7), stag...: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_11_6_other_required_systems). | [F] 903.2.11.6 Other required fire protection systems. In addition to the requirements of Section 903.2, the provisions indi- cated in Table 903.2.11.6 require the installation of a fire protection system for certain buildings and areas. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_6_other_required_systems |
| Table 903.2.11.6 | ADDITIONAL REQUIRED PROTECTION SYSTEMS | reference | draft | The sections 903.2.11.6 names, quoted by its flag (general.bc_903_2_11_6_other_required_systems). | [F] TABLE 903.2.11.6—ADDITIONAL REQUIRED PROTECTION SYSTEMS SECTION SUBJECT 402.5, 402.6.2 Covered and open mall buildings 403.3 High-rise buildings 404.3 Atriums 405.3 Underground structures 407.7 Group I-2 410.6 Stages 411.3 Special amusement buildings 412.2.4 Airport traffic control towers 412.3.6, 412.3.6.1, 412.5.6 Aircraft hangars 415.11.11 Group H-5 HPM exhaust ducts 416.5 Flammable finishes 417.4 Drying rooms 424.3 Play structures 428 Buildings containing laboratory suites 507 Unlimited area buildings 508.5.7 Live/work units 509.4 Incidental uses 1030.6.2.3 Smoke-protected assembly seating Automatic sprinkler system requirements as set forth in Section 903.2.11.6 of the Fire Code of New York FCNYS State | drafted:general.bc_903_2_11_6_other_required_systems |
| 903.2.12 | During construction | flag | draft | Sprinkler systems required during construction, alteration and demolition follow Chapter 33 of the Fire Code of New York State: a person's check (general.bc_903_2_12_during_construction). | [F] 903.2.12 During construction. Automatic sprinkler systems required during construction, alteration and demolition opera- tions shall be provided in accordance with Chapter 33 of the Fire Code of New York State. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_12_during_construction |
| 903.3.1 | Standards | reference | draft | Says every sprinkler system follows 903.3.1.1 (NFPA 13) unless 903.3.1.2 or 903.3.1.3 permits otherwise: read by each group's rule (a 13R or 13D system where not permitted isn't the system required). | [F] 903.3.1 Standards. Automatic sprinkler systems shall be designed and installed in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 unless otherwise permitted by Sections 903.3.1.2 and 903.3.1.3 and other chapters of this code, as applicable. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_1_group_a1_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_2_group_a2_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_3_group_a3_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_1_4_group_a4_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_3_group_e_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_4_group_f1_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_1_group_h_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_6_group_i_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_7_group_m_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_9_group_s1_sprinklers |
| 903.3.1.1 | NFPA 13 sprinkler systems | reference | draft | Says what a building equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 is (NFPA 13): the fact Tables 1006.2.1 and 1017.2 and 1007.1.1 turn on, stated by the architect. | [F] 903.3.1.1 NFPA 13 sprinkler systems. Where the provisions of this code require that a building or portion thereof be equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with this section, sprinklers shall be installed through- out in accordance with NFPA 13 except as provided in Sections 903.3.1.1.1 through 903.3.1.1.3. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_1_group_h_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_6_group_i_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers |
| 903.3.1.2 | NFPA 13R sprinkler systems | flag | draft | Says when a Group R occupancy may use an NFPA 13R system instead (the second sprinkler system Table 1006.2.1's note a and 1007.1.1's exception 2 accept, stated by the architect): four stories or fewer, within its heights of fire department vehicle access, which the model doesn't show; a person's check wherever a 13R system is stated or the system isn't (general.bc_903_3_1_2_nfpa_13r_limits; Chapter 9 part a, 2026-11-27). | [F] 903.3.1.2 NFPA 13R sprinkler systems. Automatic sprinkler systems in Group R occupancies shall be permitted to be installed throughout in accordance with NFPA 13R where the Group R occupancy meets all of the following conditions: | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_1_group_h_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_6_group_i_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_3_1_2_nfpa_13r_limits, drafted:general.bc_table_1006_2_1_a_e_m, drafted:general.bc_table_1006_2_1_b, drafted:general.bc_table_1006_2_1_f, drafted:general.bc_table_1006_2_1_i1_i2, drafted:general.bc_table_1006_2_1_i3, drafted:general.bc_table_1006_2_1_s, drafted:general.bc_table_1006_2_1_u, drafted:general.bc_table_1017_2_a_e_f1_m_r_s1, drafted:general.bc_table_1017_2_i1 |
| 903.3.1.2.2 | Corridors and balconies in the means of egress | flag | draft | With an NFPA 13R system, corridors and balconies in the means of egress sprinklered on five conditions the model doesn't show: a person's check (general.bc_903_3_1_2_2_corridors_balconies_13r). | [F] 903.3.1.2.2 Corridors and balconies in the means of egress. Sprinkler protection shall be provided in corridors and for balconies in the means of egress where any of the following conditions apply: 1. Corridors with combustible floor or walls. 2. Corridors with an interior change of direction exceeding 45 degrees (0.79 rad). 3. Corridors that are less than 50 percent open to the outside atmosphere at the ends. 4. Open-ended corridors and associated exterior stairways and ramps as specified in Section 1027.6, Exception 3. 5. Egress balconies not complying with Sections 1021.2 and 1021.3. | drafted:general.bc_903_3_1_2_2_corridors_balconies_13r |
| 903.3.1.3 | NFPA 13D sprinkler systems | flag | draft | Where an NFPA 13D system is permitted: dwellings, Group R-3, Group R-4 Condition 1 and townhouses; read by the groups' rules, and a person's check wherever a 13D system is stated outside Group R-3 (general.bc_903_3_1_3_nfpa_13d_scope). | [F] 903.3.1.3 NFPA 13D sprinkler systems. Automatic sprinkler systems installed in one- and two-family dwellings; Group R-3; Group R-4, Condition 1; and townhouses shall be permitted to be installed throughout in accordance with NFPA 13D. | drafted:general.bc_903_2_5_1_group_h_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_6_group_i_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_2_8_group_r_sprinklers, drafted:general.bc_903_3_1_3_nfpa_13d_scope |
| 905.3 | Required installations | flag | draft | Standpipe systems are installed where 905.3.1 to 905.3.7 require them, and may be combined with sprinkler systems (none in Group R-2 townhouses or Group R-3): a person's check (general.bc_905_3_standpipes_required). | [F] 905.3 Required installations. Standpipe systems shall be installed where required by Sections 905.3.1 through 905.3.7. Stand- pipe systems are allowed to be combined with automatic sprinkler systems. Exceptions: 1. Standpipe systems are not required in Group R-2 townhouses. 2. Standpipe systems are not required in Group R-3 occupancies. | drafted:general.bc_905_3_standpipes_required |
| 905.3.1 | Height | flag | draft | Class III standpipes throughout a building with four or more stories above or below grade plane, or its highest story's floor more than 30 feet above the lowest level of fire department vehicle access, or its lowest story's more than 30 feet below the highest (Class I allowed in a building sprinklered throughout with a 903.3.1.1 or 903.3.1.2 system, in Groups B and E, parking garages, basements sprinklered throughout, or where occupant-use hose lines won't be used by trained personnel or the fire department; recessed loading docks for four vehicles or less and impractical topography left out in finding the lowest level of access): a person's check (general.bc_905_3_1_class_iii_by_height). | [F] 905.3.1 Height. Class III standpipe systems shall be installed throughout buildings where any of the following conditions exist: 1. Four or more stories are above or below grade plane. 2. The floor level of the highest story is located more than 30 feet (9144 mm) above the lowest level of fire department vehi- cle access. 3. The floor level of the lowest story is located more than 30 feet (9144 mm) below the highest level of fire department vehi- cle access. Exceptions: 1. Class I standpipes are allowed in buildings equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system in accordance with Section 903.3.1.1 or 903.3.1.2. 2. Class I standpipes are allowed in Group B and E occupancies. 3. Class I standpipes are allowed in parking garages. 4. Class I standpipes are allowed in basements equipped throughout with an automatic sprinkler system. 5. Class I standpipes are allowed in buildings where occupant-use hose lines will not be utilized by trained personnel or the fire department. 6. In determining the lowest level of fire department vehicle access, it shall not be required to consider either of the following: 6.1. Recessed loading docks for four vehicles or less. 6.2. Conditions where topography makes access from the fire department vehicle to the building impractical or impossible. | drafted:general.bc_905_3_1_class_iii_by_height |
| 905.3.2 | Group A | flag | draft | Class I automatic wet standpipes in a nonsprinklered Group A building with an occupant load over 1,000 (not open-air seating without enclosed spaces; Class I automatic dry, semiautomatic dry or manual wet ones outside high-rise buildings): a person's check (general.bc_905_3_2_group_a_standpipes). | [F] 905.3.2 Group A. Class I automatic wet standpipes shall be provided in nonsprinklered Group A buildings having an occupant load exceeding 1,000 persons. Exceptions: 1. Open-air-seating spaces without enclosed spaces. 2. Class I automatic dry and semiautomatic dry standpipes or manual wet standpipes are allowed in buildings that are not high-rise buildings. | drafted:general.bc_905_3_2_group_a_standpipes |
| 905.3.3 | Covered and open mall buildings | flag | draft | A covered or open mall building has a standpipe system throughout where 905.3.1 requires one, else Class I hose connections on the sprinkler system delivering 250 gallons per minute at the hydraulically most remote one while also supplying the sprinkler system's demand, with at most a 50 psi residual pressure loss at that flow from the fire department connection to the hydraulically most remote hose connection, at each exit passageway or corridor entrance, each landing of interior exit stairways opening on the mall, the public entrances, and so that no part of a tenant space is more than 200 feet from one: a person's check (general.bc_905_3_3_mall_standpipes). | [F] 905.3.3 Covered and open mall buildings.
